patippuri
19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutippakutiyum, 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkappakutiyum tamiḻppulamiyum tamiḻāyvum mēlōṅki vaḷarnta poṟkālamākum. ip poṟkālap pakutiyiltāṉ tamiḻppēravic cemmal peruntamiḻaṟiñar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vāḻntu tāymoḻit tamiḻukku vaḷamum valimiyum cērttār.
ipperum pēraṟiñar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum veḷiyiṭa muṭivuceytu ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār nūl varici eṉum talippil 21 tokutikaḷ mutal kaṭṭamāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu iṉivarum kālaṅkaḷil veḷiyiṭa muyalvōm. 
 tamiḻ ici, tamiḻ ilakkiyam, tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, civa cittāntam ākiya nālvakit tuṟikaḷi miyamākak koṇṭu ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum perum payaṉtarak kūṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷākum. āḻnta camayappaṟṟāḷar, patavikkum pukaḻukkum kācukkum tammi āṭpaṭuttik koḷḷātu tamiḻppaṇi oṉṟiyē tam vāḻviṉ mūccākak koṇṭu vāḻntavar,  naṭuvaṇaracu tamiḻakattil kalavimoḻiyām intiyik (1938) kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākat tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil pukuttiyapōtu ataṉi etirttup pōrpparaṇi pāṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷil ivarum oruvar.  kākki viṭutūtu eṉum inti etirppu nūli eḻuti aṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭcik kaṭṭilil mutalvarāka amarntirunta irācācikku aṉuppit tam etirppip pativu ceytavar. 
tammuṭiya tamiḻāyvuppaṇi mūlam tamiḻ varalāṟṟil nilittu niṟpavar,  tamiḻiyum civattiyum iru kaṇkaḷeṉak koṇṭavar.   tamiḻilakkaṇat toṉṉūlām tolkāppiyatti  yum, piṉṉūlām naṉṉūliyum āḻntakaṉṟu kaṟṟu oppāyvu ceytavar, tam karuttukaḷum vāḻkki muṟiyum muraṇpaṭāmal eṇṇiyatic colli, colliyapaṭi naṭimuṟiyil vāḻntu kāṭṭiya peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟa peyar iyaṟpeyarē eṉṟu niṟuviyavar, tolkāppiyar kālattil vaṭakkē vēṅkaṭamalittoṭarum, teṟkē kumariyāṟum tamiḻaka ellikaḷāka amintiruntaṉaveṉṟum, kaṭalkōḷukkup piṟaku kumarikkaṭal teṉ elli āṉatu eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyar iṭiccaṅkak kālattavar, tolkāppiyam iṭiccaṅkak kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, muccaṅka varalāṟṟi mutaṉmutalil kūṟiyatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uritāṉ eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyam, caṅkac ceyyuḷukkum tirukkuṟaḷukkum neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, tirumūlar tam tirumantiramē cittānta cāttiram patiṉāṉkiṟkum mutal nūlāka tikaḻvatu eṉpatiyum, tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ mutalām irācarācaṉ alla mutalām ātittaṉē  tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ eṉpatiyum,  vaḷḷalāriṉ tiruvaruṭpā tamiḻiṉ colvaḷamum, poruḷ nuṭpamum, oppaṟṟap pēraruḷiṉ iṉpamum niṟintatu  eṉpatiyum, civa camayam āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, paḻantamiḻ nākarikattiṉ ūṟṟukkaṇ tamiḻum civamum eṉpatiyum tam nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi uṟuti ceytavar. tam āyvup pulamiyāl pala putiya ceytikaḷiyum tamiḻ ulakukku aḷittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻulakiṟkup perumi cērppaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟiṟku  kūṭutal varavāka amivaṉa. ivaruṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷa ṉittiyum āvaṇappaṭuttum   nōkkuṭaṉ tokuttu tamiḻ ulakiṟku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. itaṉi veḷikkoṇara emakkut tuṇiyāyirunta em patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ, nūlkaḷ koṭuttu utaviyavarkaḷ,  kaṇiṉi, meyppu, accu, nūl kaṭṭamippu ceytu innūlvarici ceppamuṭaṉ  veḷivarat tuṇiniṉṟa aṉivarukkum naṉṟi.  em tamiḻk kāppup paṇikku tuṇi niṟka vēṇṭukiṟōm.
2010	 patippakattār




tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm!
tamiḻārāycciyiṉ vaḷarcciyil putiya pōkkukaḷi uṇṭākkiya perumikkuriyavarkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip patippitta aṟiñar kaḷēyāvar.  ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil irunta ilakkiya, ilakkaṇapperuñ celvaṅkaḷi aṉivarum aṟiyumāṟu ceytu putiya āyviṟkut taṭam patittavarkaḷ ivarkaḷē āvar.
mēli nāṭṭār varukiyiṉāl tōṉṟiya acciyantira vacatikaḷum, kalvi maṟumalarcciyum putiya nūlākkaṅkaḷukku vaḻi vakuttaṉa. āṟumuka nāvalar (1822-1879) ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi (1832-1901), u.vē. cāminātiyar (1855-1942) ākiyōr tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷāy viḷaṅkit tamiḻukku vaḷam cērttaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakattiṉ mēṉāḷ tuṇivēntar, i. cuntaramūrtti taṉatu patippiyal cintaṉikaḷ eṉum nūlil pativu ceytuḷḷār. innūl tokutikaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm.

tolkāppiyam
poruḷatikāram
kaṟpiyal
1. 	kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē.
eṉpatu cūttiram.
iḷampūraṇam :	ivvōttu eṉṉapeyarttō veṉiṉ, kaṟpiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. kaṟpukku ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉami yāṟ peṟṟa peyar. kaṟpeṉpatu yātōveṉiṉ, aḵtāmāṟu iccūttirattil viḷaṅkum.
iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ kaṟpilakkaṇam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(itaṉ poruḷ) kaṟpeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu, karaṇattōṭu poruntik koḷḷutaṟkuriya marapiṉiyuṭiya kiḻavaṉ koḷḷutaṟ kuriya marapiṉiyuṭiya kiḻattiyik koṭuttaṟkuriya marapiṉi yuṭiyār koṭuppak koḷvatu eṉṟavāṟu.
koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ eṉpataṉik kiḻatti eṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭiyurikka.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ ottāriruvar vēṭki mikutiyāṟ kūṭi oḻukiyavaḻik karaṇattiṉ amiyātu illaṟam naṭattalāmō eṉiṉ, aḵtākāteṉṟaṟkuk karaṇamoṭu puṇara eṉṟār. karaṇam eṉpatu vatuvic caṭaṅku. koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavōṉ eṉṟataṉāl ottakulattāṉum (uyarnta kulattāṉum) eṉṟu koḷka. koḷaṟkuriya marapiṟ kiḻatti yeṉṟataṉāl, otta kulattāḷum iḻinta kulattāḷum eṉṟu koḷka. koṭikkuriya marapiṉōr eṉṟataṉāl. tantiyum tāyum taṉṉiyarum mātulaṉum ivarillātavaḻic cāṉṟōrum teyvamum eṉṟu koḷka. koṭuppak koḷvatu kaṟpu eṉṟamiyāl, atu koṭukkuṅkāl, kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa vaḻiyum, kaḷavu veḷippaṭāta vaḻiyum, meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉṟi uḷḷappuṇarcciyāṉ urimi pūṇṭa vaḻiyum koḷḷappeṟum eṉakkoḷka. kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattuḷ,
		iṉpamum poruḷu maṟaṉu meṉṟāṅ
kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta	(kaḷaviyal. 1)
eṉpataṉit tanturittu, aintiṇi  maruṅkiṟ kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu eṉak kūṭṭuka.1
aḵtēl, koṭuppak koḷvatu kaṟpāyiṉ piramam mutaliya eṇ vakiyum koḷka. koṭuppō riṉṟiyum karaṇa muṇṭē--- puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa eṉṉum itu kaṟpākumō eṉiṉ, aviyum kaṟpātal okkumēṉum kantiruvampōla otta aṉpuṭiyār ātal orutaliyaṉmiyiṉ kikkiḷi perun tiṇippāṟpaṭum. īṇṭu aintiṇi taḻuviya akattiṇiyiyē kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉap pakuttār eṉṟu koḷka.2
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttuk kaḷavu kaṟpeṉṉuṅ kikōḷiraṇṭiṉuṭ kaṟpuṇarttiṉamiyiṟ kaṟpiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. kaṟpiyal kaṟpiṉatu iyaleṉa virikka. iyal, ilakkaṇam. aḵtu ākupeyarāṉ ōttiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu. atu koṇṭāṉiṟ ciṟanta teyvam iṉṟeṉavum. avaṉi iṉṉavāṟē vaḻipaṭukaveṉavum irumutukuravar kaṟpittalāṉum, antaṇartiṟattuñ cāṉṟōr tēettum aiyar pāṅkiṉum amararc cuṭṭiyum (tol. po. 146) oḻukum oḻukkan talimakaṉ kaṟpittalāṉuṅ kaṟpāyiṟṟu. iṉit talivaṉuṅ kaḷaviṉkaṇ ōriyum nāḷun tīteṉṟu ataṉit tuṟantoḻukiṉāṟ pōla oḻukātu ōttiṉuṅ karaṇattiṉum yātta ciṟappilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kaṟpittukkoṇṭu tuṟavaṟattiṟ cellun tuṇiyum illaṟa nikaḻttutaliṟ kaṟpāyiṟṟu. kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉarāyiṉum atu veḷippaṭāmiyāyiṉum uḷḷap puṇarcci nikaḻnta vaḻiyāyiṉum varital ak kaḷaviṉ vaḻi yātaliṉ mēlataṉōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram kaṟpiṟkellām potu vilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
.	kaṟpu eṉappaṭuvatu---kaṟpeṉṟuciṟappittu¡kūṟappaṭuvatu;karaṇamoLpuṇara---vēḷvi¢caṭaṅkōṭē1kūṭa;koḷaṟFuÇmarapi‰kiḻavaṉ---otjkulattōṉu«mikfkula¤tōṉumāki¡koḷḷutaṟkuriamuṟimiyiṉiyuṭiatalivaṉ;kiḻattiyi---otj kulattāḷum iḻinta kulattāḷumākiya taliviyi; . crikrapiṉōrbkāṭuppa-koṭuttaṟFriyaKṟimiyiṉiyuṭiyaïrumutukuravarKtalāyiṉārbkāṭuppa;bkāḷvatu---
kōṭaṟṟoḻil(e-ṟu.)

eṉappaṭuvatu 
. peyar koḷvatu eṉṉum peyarp payaṉili koṇṭatu: itu ciṟappuṇarttutal avvac colliṟku (tol. col. iṭi. 47) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuṭ kūṟiṉāṉ. koṭuppō riṉṟiyum (tol. po. 143) eṉa mēl varukiṉṟatākaliṉ ik kaṟpuc ciṟattaliṟ ciṟanta teṉṟār. iḵtu eṉa eṉkiṉṟa eccamātaliṟ collaḷavē eñciniṉṟatu. itaṉāṟ karaṇam piḻikkil maraṇam payakku meṉṟār.1 at toḻiliṉ nikaḻuṅkāl ivaḷi iṉṉavāṟu pātukāppāyeṉavum, ivaṟku iṉṉavāṟē nī kuṟṟēval ceytoḻukeṉavum aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ aṟikariyāka mantira vakiyāṟ kaṟpikkappaṭutaliṉ at toḻilik kaṟpeṉṟār. talivaṉ pātukāvātu parattimi ceytu oḻukiṉum piṉṉar atu kiviṭṭu illaṟamē nikaḻttit tuṟavaṟattē celvaṉeṉ ṟuṇarka. ik kaṟpukkāraṇamākavē piṉṉar nikaḻnta oḻukalāṟellām nikaḻavēṇṭutaliṉ avaṟṟiyuṅ kaṟpeṉṟu aṭakkiṉār. iruvarum etirppaṭṭa ñāṉṟu toṭaṅki uḻuvalaṉ pāl urimi ceytu oḻukaliṟ kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyum eṉṟār. tāyoṭu piṟantārun taṉṉiyarun tāyattārum ācāṉum mutaliyōr koṭikkuriyar eṉṟaṟku marapiṉōr eṉṟār.2
c.m :	uḻuntu talippeyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavi	(akam.86)
itaṉuḷ vatuvikku ēṟṟa karaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻntavāṟum tamar koṭuttavāṟum kāṇka. cuṟṟañ cūḻntu niṟṟalāṉun tamar aṟiya maṇavaṟic cēṟalāṉuṅ kaḷavāṟ curuṅkiniṉṟa nāṇ ciṟantamiyip piṉṉart talivaṉ viṉāva avaḷ maṟumoḻi koṭātu niṉṟamiyit talivaṉ tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. itaṉāṉē itu kaḷaviṉvaḻi nikaḻnta kaṟpāyiṟṟu1.
āyvuri :	kaṟpiṉatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyāl itu kaṟpiyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. aṉpurimi pūṇṭu kaḷa voḻukkam oḻukiya talivaṉ. taṉpāl aṉpuṭiya taliviyi maṇañceytu koṭuttaṟku urimiyuṭiya cuṟṟattār koṭuppa ulakaṟiya maṇantu vāḻum maṉivāḻkkiyē kaṟpeṉṉum oḻukalāṟākum. muṉṉark kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟiya vaṇṇam otta aṉpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl ōriṭat tetirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyoḻukiṉārāyiṉum irumutukuravar uṭaṉpāṭiṉṟi avviruvarum ulakaṟiya maṉivāḻkkiyi mēṟkoḷḷutal iyalātu. eṉavē kātalar iruvarum oruvari yoruvar piriyātu vāḻtaṟkuriya tamatu uḷḷattuṟutiyiṉi ulakattāraṟiya veḷip paṭuttum niyatiyākiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkuṭaṉ talivaṉ taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷutal maṉivāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta ciṟappuṭiya nikaḻcciyāyiṟṟu.
kaḷavoḻukkattil urimipūṇṭaṭoḻukiya talivaṉum taliviyum ulakattār aṟiya maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttutaṟku urimi ceytaḷikkum ceyalmuṟikaḷē paṇṭittamiḻar koṇṭoḻukiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākum. itaṉik karaṇam eṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkuvar tolkāppiyaṉār.
kaṇavaṉiṟ ciṟanta teyvam illiyeṉavum avaṉi iṉṉavāṟu vaḻipaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉavum talimakaḷukkup peṟṟōr kaṟpittalāṉum. antaṇar cāṉṟōr aruntavattōr viruntiṉar mutaliyōrpāl iṉṉavāṟu naṭantu koḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉat talivaṉ talivikkuk kaṟpittalāṉum, niṉmaṉiviyi iṉṉavāṟu pātukāppāyāka eṉat talivaṉukkum, niṉkaṇavaṉukku ivvāṟu paṇiceytoḻukuvāyāka eṉat talivikkum cāṉṟōr kaṟpittalāṉum, iṉit talivaṉum kaḷaviṉkaṇ ōriyum nāḷun tīteṉṟa eṇṇattiṉit tuṟantoḻukiṉāṟpōla oḻukātu ōttiṉum karaṇattiṉum yātta ciṟappilakkaṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalāṉum ittirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇamum kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
talivaṉ talivi iruvarum oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarāy maṇantu vāḻuṅkāl talimakaḷatu maṉattiṉ kaṇ aminta kalaṅkānilimiyākiya tiṇmiyē kaṟpeṉappaṭum eṉpatu tamiḻ muṉṉōr tuṇipākum.
		peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉun
tiṇmiyuṇ ṭākap peṟiṉ	(tirukkuṟaḷ-54)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ittakiya maṉattiṇmiyiṉiyum talivaṉatu niṟiyiṉiyum ulakattār aṟiyap pulappaṭuttuvatē tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇamākum. kātalar iruvarum īruṭaṟku ōruyireṉap piriviṉṟiyiyinta naṭpuṭiyār eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttuvatu   ittakiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇamēyātaliṉ, inniyati piḻi paṭumēl avviruvaratu vāḻkkiyilum cātaliyotta peruntuṉpam nērum eṉpatu tiṇṇam. karaṇam piḻikkil maraṇam payakkum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭuttukkāṭṭiya paḻamoḻi karaṇattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi valiyuṟuttal kāṇka.
itu, kaṟpeṉṉum oḻukalāṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kaṟpeṉṟu collappaṭum oḻukalāṟāvatu, karaṇat toṭu porunti, maṇantu koḷḷutaṟkuriya iyalpiṉi yuṭiya talivaṉ taṉakku urimiyuṭiya taliviyik koṭuttaṟkuriya cuṟṟattār koṭuppa ēṟṟu maṇañceytu koḷḷum muṟimiyākum e-ṟu.
kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu eṉṉum eḻuvāy, koḷvatu eṉṉum peyarppayaṉili koṇṭatu. koḷḷutaṟkuriya marapāvatu, kātalar iruvarkkiṭiyē amiya vēṇṭiya piṟappu mutaliya paṇpukaḷāṟ poruntit talimakaḷāṟ kātalikkap peṟum keḻutakimiyākiya urimiyuṭiyaṉātal. ittakiya vurimiyuṭiya talivaṉē taliviyi maṇantu koḷḷutaṟkuriya takutiyuṭiyāṉ eṉa vitikkum muṟiyil koḷaṟkurimarapiṟ kiḻavaṉ eṉa aṭipuṇarttōtiṉār. kiḻattiyi eṉpatu iṭinili viḷakkāka niṉṟu kiḻattiyik koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavaṉ eṉavum, kiḻattiyik koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppa eṉavum īriṭattuñ ceṉṟiyintatu. maṉiyaṟattiṉi nikaḻttaṟkuriya oruvaṉ orutti yeṉṉum iruvarum oruvari yoruvar aṉpiṉāl iṉṟiyamiyātavarāka uḷḷam ottu oḻukum keḻutakimiyākiya uṇarvurimiyē avviruvarum ulakattāraṟiyat tirumaṇam purintu koḷḷutaṟkuriya takutiyām eṉa vaṟpuṟuttum niliyil avviruvariyum kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉṟa peyarāṟ kiḷantu ōtiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār. eṉavē oruvaṉ oruttiyeṉṉum iruvarum tammuḷ otta kātaṟkiḻami yuṭiyarāyiṉaṉṟit tirumaṇam ceytukōṭaṟ kuriyarallar eṉpatum, kātalar iruvar icivuṭaṉ makaṭkoṭikkuriyarākiya taliviyiṉ peṟṟōratu icivum oruṅkē kiṭippiṉ ulakattār at tirumaṇattiṉi aiyattiṟkiṭaṉiṉṟit teḷintu uṭaṉpaṭuvar eṉpatum itaṉāṟ pulaṉātal kāṇalām.              (1)
2.	koṭuppōr iṉṟiyuṅ karaṇa muṇṭē
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli ahd.1
iḷampūraṇam :	itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ) koṭuppōriṉṟiyum, karaṇa nikaḻcci uṇṭu; puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kālattu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē kaṟpiṟkuk karaṇa nikaḻcci orutaliyāyiṟṟu, itaṉāṉē koṭuppō rilvaḻiyum karaṇanikaḻcci yuṇmiyum oḻukkak kuṟipāṭu iṉmiyum koḷka.
paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoḷpu
toṉmū tālattup potiyil tōṉṟiya
nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla
vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi yāykaḻaṟ
cēyili veḷvēl viṭaliyoṭu
tokuvaḷi muṉki maṭanti naṭpē.	(kuṟun. 15)
itaṉuḷ viṭaliyoṭu maṭantinaṭpu paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoṇṭu nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla vāyāyiṟṟu eṉac cevili naṟṟāykkuk kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, viṭali eṉap pāli nilattiṟkuriya talivaṉ peyar kūṟiṉa miyāṉum koṭuppōriṉṟiyum karaṇam nikaḻntavāṟu kāṇka.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu 
viti.
(i-ḷ.)	koṭuppōr1 iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē--- muṟkūṟiya koṭikkuriya marapiṉōr koṭuppak kōṭaliṉṟiyuṅ karaṇam uṇṭākum; puṇarntu uṭaṉ pōkiya kāliyāṉa--- òṇarntucṭaṉnpākiyaflattiṭattu(e.ṟu).ïJ puṇarntu uṭaṉpōyiṉār āṇṭuk koṭup pōriṉṟiyum vēḷvi yācāṉ kāṭṭiya caṭaṅkiṉ vaḻiyāṟ kaṟpup pūṇṭu varuvatum āmeṉṟavāṟu. iṉi āṇṭu variyātu mīṇṭuvantu koṭuppak kōṭal cḷatēlmtunmaṟkūṟiyajṉkaṇmṭaṅkumïṉipnpāyavaḻik2fṟpupóṇṭalēfraṇamvṉpārumuḷar.vdnt kaṟpiṟkuk karaṇam orutaliyāyiṟṟu.
paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa ... ... maṭanti naṭpē 1	 (kuṟun. 15)
itaṉuḷ vāyākiṉṟu eṉac cevili naṟṟāykkuk  kūṟiṉami yāṉum viṭali yeṉap pāli nilattut talivaṉ peyar kūṟiṉamiyāṉum itu koṭuppōriṉaṟik karaṇam nikaḻntatu. aruñcura miṟantaveṉ peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ (akam 195) eṉpatum atu.
āyvuri :	itu, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i.ḷ)	makaṭkoṭikkuriya peṟṟōratu uṭaṉpāṭu illāta niliyilum tirumaṇa nikaḻcciyākiya karaṇam nikaḻtal uṇṭu. (aḵtu eppoḻutu eṉiṉ) kaḷavoḻukkattil talivi taṉ kātalaṉākiya talivaṉoṭu peṟṟōr aṟiyātavāṟu uṭaṉ pōkkiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kālattu e-ṟu.
kaṟpiyal vāḻvukkut tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇam iṉṟiyamiyātateṉpatum. makaṭkoṭikkuriya peṟṟōratu uṭaṉpāṭu peṟāta niliyilum tammuḷ otta aṉpiṉarākiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum tammuḷ tirumaṇam purintu maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttuvatil taṭiyilliyeṉpatum. otta aṉpiṉarākiya iruvarum tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷutaṟku ulakattuc cāṉṟōr aṉivarum tuṇiceyvar eṉpatum itaṉāṟ pulaṉātal kāṇka. kāliyāṉa-kālattu. kāli-kālam. āṉ eṉṉum mūṉṟāmurupu akaram peṟṟu āṉeṉat tirintu iṭapporuḷ tantu niṉṟatu.2
3.	mēlōr mūvarkkum puṇartta karaṇaṅ
kīḻōrk kākiya kālamum uṇṭē.
iḷampūraṇam :	ituvumatu.
(i -ḷ.)	mēṟkulattārākiya antaṇar aracar vaṇikar eṉṉum mūṉṟu varuṇarttārkkum puṇartta karaṇam kīḻōrākiya vēḷāṇmāntarkkum ākiya kālamum uṇṭu e - ṟu.
itaṉāṟ colliyatu, muṟkālattukkaraṇam potuppaṭa nikaḻtaliṉellārkkumāmeṉpatum,piṟkālattuvēḷāṇmāntarkkuttavirntateṉavuṅTṟiyavāṟunpālum1.m~jhkhW jrumacāttiramtllārikkoṇLṇarka.e¢ádh®¡»Åa« :	itu mutalūḻiyil vēḷāḷarkku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i -ḷ.)	mēlōr mūvarkkum puṇartta karaṇam-vētanūltāṉ antaṇar aracarvaṇikareṉṉu«mūvarkku«uriyavāka¡kūṟiakaraṇam:kīḻōrkFākiakālamu«uṇṭu-antaṇa®mutaliyōrkku«maka£koṭikkuriavēḷā©māntarkkuªtantiumantiuvakiyā‹urittākiakālamu«uḷa. (e - ṟu.)
eṉavē, mutaṟkālattu nāṉku varuṇattārkkuṅ karaṇam oṉṟāy nikaḻntatu eṉpatām. aḵtu iraṇṭām ūḻi toṭaṅki vēḷāḷarkkut tavirntatu eṉpatūun taliccaṅkattārum mutaṉūlāciriyar kūṟiya muṟiyē karaṇam oṉṟākac ceyyuḷ ceytār eṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. utāraṇam ik kālattiliṉṟu1.
āyvuri :	itu, paṇṭittamiḻakattil mutaṉmutaṟ karaṇam vakukkappeṟṟōr iṉṉār eṉavum avarkkuriya karaṇam ēṉiyōrkkum urittākiya kālam ituveṉavum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nāṭāḷum mēṉmiyuṭiyōrākiya muṭivēntar mūvarkkum mutaṉmutal vakukkappeṟṟa tirumaṇa niyatiyākiya karaṇam avarkīḻvāḻum kuṭimakkaḷukkum uriyatāy aminta kālamum uṇṭu. e-ṟu.
itaṉkaṇ mēlōrmūvar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭōr. pōnti vēmpē ār eṉa varūum mā peruntaṉiyar eṉap puṟattiṇiyiyalilum vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippu eṉac ceyyuḷiyalilum ciṟappitturikkappaṭṭa cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷākiya muṭivēntar mūvarumē eṉak koḷḷutal ēṟpuṭiyatākum. karaṇam eṉappaṭum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkumuṟi mikap paḻiya kālattil nāṭṭil ellā makkaḷukkum vitikkap peṟavilli; paṭippukkālan toṭṭup paṇpuṭaṉ nilipeṟṟu varum tamiḻmūvēntar kuṭumpattiṟkē mutaṉmutal vakukkap peṟṟatu. nāṭāḷvēntarākiya avartam āṭciyurimiyil maṉṉa ṉukkuppiṉ avaṉ mintar aracurimi peṟum muṟiyil ammaṉṉaṉatu paṭṭattaraci mutaṉmi peṟutal kāraṇamākavē muṭivēntar mūvarkkum mutaṉmutaṟ karaṇam vakukkappeṟṟatu.
oruvaṉ orutti yiṭiyēyaminta kātaṟkēṇmi nāḷaṭiviṟ kuṟintu vaḻuvāmi karutiyum, peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruṭku uriyarām tāyamuṟi avartam makkaṭkēyuriyateṉat tuṇitaṟ poruṭṭum muṟkālattil muṭivēntar mūvarkkum vakukkappeṟṟa tirumaṇa niyatiyākiya karaṇam avarkīḻvāḻum kuṭimakkaḷukkum piṟkālattil uriyatāyiṟṟu eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār tamkālac camutāya oḻukalāṟṟiṉi in-nūṟpāvāṟ pulappaṭuttiya tiṟam kūrntuṇarattakuvatākum. (3)
4.	poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.
iḷampūraṇam :	eṉṟatu, karaṇamākiyavāṟu uṇarttutal  nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.)	poykūṟalum vaḻūuppaṭa voḻukalum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar muṉivar karaṇattik kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟu colvar e - ṟu.
iraṇṭun tōṉṟuvatu iraṇṭām ūḻiyiṉkaṇṇātaliṉ. mutalūḻiyiṟ karaṇamiṉṟiyē ivvāḻkki naṭantateṉpatūum ivi tōṉṟiya piṉṉark karaṇan tōṉṟiṉa teṉpatūum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. poyyāvatu ceytataṉi maṟittal vaḻuvāvatu ceytataṉkaṇ muṭiya nillātu tappi yoḻukutal. karaṇattoṭu muṭinta kāliyil avi yiraṇṭum nikaḻāvā mātalāṟ karaṇam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu.1
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu vētattiṟ karaṇam oḻiya āriṭamākiya karaṇam piṟantavāṟum ataṟkuk kāraṇamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.2
(i-ḷ.)	poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉar - āti ūḻi kaḻinta muṟiyē akkālattantan toṭaṅki iraṇṭām ūḻi mutalākap poyyum vaḻuvuñ ciṟantu tōṉṟiya piṟkālattē; aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa - iruṭikaḷ mēlōr karaṇamum kīḻōr karaṇamum vēṟupaṭak kaṭṭiṉāreṉṟu kūṟuvar. (e - ṟu).
īṇṭu eṉpa (249) eṉṟatu mutaṉūlāciriyariyaṉṟu, vaṭa nūlōrik karutiyatu. poyyāvatu ceyta oṉṟaṉic ceytilēṉeṉṟal; vaḻuvāvatu collutalē aṉṟi oḻukkattu iḻukki oḻukal. aḵtu aracarum vāṇikarun tattam vakiyāṟ ceyyattakuvaṉa ceyyātu caṭaṅkoppumi karutit tāmum antaṇarōṭu talimi ceytoḻukutaluṅ kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ iḻukkutal pōlvaṉavum avarkkiḻukkam. ēṉi vēḷāḷarum iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉṉarp poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟi vaḻuvutal avarkkiḻukkam. ivaṟṟik kaṇṭu iruṭikaḷ1 mēlōr mūvarkkum vēṟu vēṟu caṭaṅkiṉik kaṭṭik kīḻōrkkuṅ kaḷaviṉṟiyum kaṟpu nikaḻumeṉavuñ caṭaṅku vēṟu vēṟu kaṭṭiṉār eṉavē, oruvar kaṭṭāmal tāmē tōṉṟiya karaṇam vētanūṟkē uḷateṉpatu peṟṟām. āyiṉ kantaruva vaḻakkattiṟkuc ciṟanta kaḷavu vilakkuṇṭataṉṟō eṉiṉ, oruvaṉiyum oruttiyiyum etirniṟīi ivaḷik koḷḷa iyitiyō nī eṉavum, ivaṟkuk koṭuppa iyitiyō nī eṉavum irumutukuravarum kēṭṭavaḻi avar karanta uḷḷattāṉ iyinta vaḻik koṭuppa vākaliṉ atutāṉē oruvakiyāṟ kantaruva vaḻakkamām, kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻātāyiṉum eṉpatu, karaṇam yāttōr karutteṉpatu peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiyum uḷḷappuṇarcciyeṉṟu kūṟi ataṉ vaḻik kaṟpu nikaḻntateṉṟuṅ kūṟavumpaṭum. ivvāciriyar āti ūḻiyiṉ antattē1 in nūl ceytaliṉ mutaṉūlāciriyar kūṟiyavāṟē kaḷavu nikaḻntapiṉṉark kaṟpu nikaḻumāṟuṅ kūṟit tām nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattup poyyum vaḻuvum paṟṟi iruṭikaḷ karaṇam yāttavāṟuṅ kūṟiṉār; ak kaḷaviṉ vaḻi nikaḻnta kaṟpuṅ kōṭaṟkeṉṟu uṇarka.

irumpāl kūntal iruṇmaṟi yoḷittē	(akam.136)1
eṉa varum.
āyvuri :	itu, karaṇat tōṟṟattiṟkuriya kāraṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ) oruvaṉ oruttiyi aṉpiṉāṟ kūṭiyoḻukip piṉ avaḷi aṟiyēṉ eṉap poy kūṟutalum, niṉṉippiriyēṉ eṉat teyvattiṉ muṉṉiliyil uṟutikūṟip piṉṉar avvuṟutiyiṉ vaḻuvi naṭattalum ākiya tīya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē cāṉṟōrākiya talivarkaḷ kātalar iruvarum kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumākap piriviṉṟi vāḻtaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇattiṉi vakuttamittaṉar e-ṟu.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar aiyar karaṇam yāt taṉar eṉavē. avi tōṉṟāta kālam mika muntiyateṉpatum, at toṉmik kālattil ittakiya tirumaṇa niyatiyākiya karaṇattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāmi nērntatilliyeṉpatum, aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa eṉattolkāppiyaṉār tam muṉṉōr kūṟṟāka vitturittalāl ittakiya karaṇamākiya niyati āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattiṟku muṉṉarē tamiḻ muṉṉōrākiya talivarkaḷāl vitikkap peṟṟu vaḻaṅki vanta teṉpatum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum.						          (4)
5.	karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭinta kāli
neñcutaḷi aviḻnta puṇarccik kaṇṇum
eñcā makiḻcci iṟantuvaru paruvattum
añca vanta urimik kaṇṇum
naṉṉeṟip paṭarun toṉṉalap poruḷiṉum
peṟṟa tēettup perumiyiṉ niliik
kuṟṟañ cāṉṟa poruḷeṭut turippiṉum
nāmak kālat tuṇṭeṉat tōḻi
ēmuṟu kaṭavuḷ ēttiya maruṅkiṉum
allal tīra ārvamo ṭaḷiic
colluṟu poruḷiṉ kaṇṇuñ colleṉa
ēṉatu cuvippiṉum nīki toṭṭatu
vāṉōr amiḻtam puriyumāl emakkeṉa
aṭicilum pūvun toṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum1
antaṇar tiṟattum cāṉṟōr tēettum
antamil ciṟappiṟ piṟarpiṟar tiṟattiṉum
oḻukkaṅ kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉum oḻukkattuk

paṇṇami pakuti muppatiṉoru mūṉṟum- ōtuppaṭṭa iviyē iṭamāka nallaṟivuṭiyōr āṇṭāṇṭu vēṟuvēṟākac ceyyuḷ ceytu kōṭaṟku amintuniṉṟa kūṟupāṭṭi uṭiyavākiya muppattumūṉṟu tuṟiyum:3 
eṇṇañ ciṟappiṟ kiḻavōṉ mēṉa -kaḷavupōla iḻitoḻiliṉṟi ārāytaṟkariya ciṟappōṭukūṭiya talivaṉ kaṇṇa eṉṟavāṟu. 1 
ciṟappāvaṉa:- vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukalum, illaṟam nikaḻttalum, pirivāṟṟutalum, piṟavumām. iṉṉaviṭattum iṉṉaviṭattum nikaḻuṅ kūṟṟukkaḷi vāyiletir kūṟuṅ kūṟṟōṭē tokuttup paṇṇutaṟkaminta pakutiyuṭiyavākiya muppattumūṉṟu tuṟiyun talivaṉkaṇ nikaḻvaṉa eṉṟu muṭikka. eṭutturippiṉun tannilikiḷappiṉum ak kūṟṟukkaḷiyum vāyiletiroṭu tokii yeṉa muṭikka. ivaṟṟuṭ paṇṇik koḷḷum pakutiyāvaṉa, yām maṟintu ceṉṟu ivaṉik kaṇṇip putittāl taliniṉṟoḻukum parattiyar peyar kūṟuvaṉeṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu kāmakkiḻattiyātal taliviyātal ceṉṟu kaṇputittuḻit talivaṉ kūṟuvaṉavum, paḷḷiyiṭattu vantiruntu kūṟuvaṉavum, ivaḷ ūṭaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉeṉṟu tōḻi viṉāyavaḻik kūṟuvaṉavum, pirintakālattu ivaḷi maṟantavāṟeṉṉeṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟuvaṉavum,piṟavumām. 2

eṉavarum. iṉṉuṅ kaṟpiyaṟkaṇ talivaṉkūṟṟāy vēṟupaṭa varuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷellām itaṉāṉ amittuk koḷka.
āyvuri :	itu, maṉivāḻkkiyil talivaṉukkuriya kiḷavikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) maṉivāḻkkikku iṉṟiyāmiyāta karaṇattuṭaṉ  tirumaṇam niṟivēṟiya kālattu, accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum perumiyum uraṉum kāraṇamākat tammuḷḷattu vēṭki tōṟṟātavāṟu muṉṉark kaṭṭuṇṭirunta iruvar neñcamum kaṭṭaviḻntu malarntu kūṭiya varinteytuṅ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇṇum, taṉṉuḷḷattu nīṅkāta makiḻcci mikkut tōṉṟuṅ kālattiṉum, añcum vaṇṇam talimakaḷiṭattē uḷatākiya kaṟpurimiyākiya nalattiṉ kaṇṇum, illaṟamākiya naṉṉeṟik kaṇṇē oḻukutaṟku ētuvākiya toṉmi vāynta naṟpaṇpukaḷākiya poruḷiṉ kaṇṇum, talimakaḷi varintu peṟṟaviṭattut taliviyi maṉimāṭciyākiya perumiyiṉkaṇ niṟuttik kaḷavuk kālattuk kuṟṟam poruntiya poruṇmiyi eṭuttukkūṟum nilimiyiṉum, vaḻiyil nikaḻum palvēṟu iṭiyūṟukaḷiyeṇṇi añcutaṟkētuvākiya kaḷavuk kālattu (tōṉṟāttuṇiyāy utaviya teyvam) uṇṭu eṉṟu tōḻi tamakkup pātukāvalāyuḷḷa kaṭavuḷi ēttiya iṭattum, (talivi taṉ) tuṉpantīra ārvattuṭaṉ kalantu collutaluṟṟa poruḷilum, (taliviyāṟ kiṭikkappeṟṟa) yātāyiṉum ōruṇaviṉi nukarumiṭattu niṉkiyāl amikkap peṟṟa itu emakkut tēvar amiḻtattiṉi ottu aṇṇikkiṉṟatu, ataṟkuriya kāraṇam kūṟuvāyāka eṉa aṭiciliyum pūviyum toṭutaṟkaṇṇum, aṟavōrākiya antaṇariṭattum cāṉṟōriṭattum keṭāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya ēṉiyōr piṟariṭattum iṉṉavāṟu oḻukutal vēṇṭum eṉat talivikkuk kuṟippiṉāl aṟivuṟuttumiṭattum, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉuḷ oruvari yoruvar eḷitiṟ kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkiyalātavāṟu nikaḻnta arumippāṭṭiṉi niṉintu taṉittuc cuḻaṉṟavuḷḷattōṭu kalanturiyāṭumiṭattum, kaḷavukkālattut tampāl nikaḻnta kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ākāyattil eḻutiya eḻuttuppōṉṟu avinikaḻnta iṭamum tōṉṟātu maṟakkap paṭṭuk keṭṭoḻiyumāṟu oruvarkkoruvar aṉpu mītūra oḻukutaṟkaṇṇum, taṉatu pirivāṟṟātu varuntutalum tāṉ varum vaḻiyiṉ iṭiyūṟu niṉintu añcutalum eṉa avviru nilimikkaṇ tāṉ talivikkuk kuṟitta vaṇṇam vārātu tappiyoḻukiya kālattum, poṟiyum perumiyum oruvaṭivu koṇṭāṟpōṉṟu taṇṇaḷiyuṭiyaḷāyp pukaḻ niṟinta tōḻimutaliya vāyiloṭuporuntit taṉ pōṉṟa putalvaṉi yīṉṟeṭutta talimakaḷatu takutik kaṇṇum, putalvaṉippeṟṟu īṉṟaṇimi nīṅkiya poḻutiṉkaṇ neyyāṭal purinta taliviyi nōkki avaḷuṭiya tamiyaṉmār pakkattārāka avarkaḷik kuṟittum amarpurintu teyvanili peṟṟa taṉ kuṭimutalvarākiya vīrarkaḷikkuṟittum ceyyappaṭum ciṟappuṭiya nikaḻcciyiṟ kalantu koḷḷumiṭattum, puṟattoḻukkam kāraṇamākat taṉṉikkūṭum viruppiṉṟip payaṉ poruntiya aṇiyiṉip pulli varuntikkiṭanta taliviyi aṇuki avaḷoṭu taṅkutalik karutiya niṟikaṭanta niliyil taliviyiṉmeṉmi poruntiya ciṟiya aṭikaḷip paṟṟik koṇṭu irattaṟkaṇṇum, tāṉ neruṅkutaṟkiyalātavāṟu ūṭalmutirnta taliviyi avaḷoṭu paḻakiya vevvēṟu peṇṭir mūlamāka ūṭali nīkkutaṟ kaṇṇum, taṉpōṉṟu pirivukāraṇamākat taṉimiyuṟṟu varuntiya kātalar iruvariyum avvaruttattiṉiṉṟu nīkkiya tiṟattiṉkaṇṇum, nāṭiṭiyiṭṭup piriyum neṭuvaḻip piriviṉkaṇ añciya varuttattiṉkaṇṇum. mēṟkūṟiya neṭuvaḻiyiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟu mīṇṭum avvaḻiyiṉkaṇ tirumpa niṉittaviṭattum, poruḷiviṭak kāmam valiyuṭittākiya taṉmiyi uḷaṅkoṇṭaniliyilum, taliviyippirintavaḻi avaṭku nērum tuyarkaruti añciya niliyilum, niṉṉiṟpiriyēṉ eṉat talimakaṭkuc colliya collit tāṉ tappiya niliyilum, taliviyi uṭaṉaḻittuc cellutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum ceytiyuṭaṉ avipōlvaṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum taṉ maṭamik kuṇantōṉṟak kūṟiya tōḻiyiṉiṭattum, tāṉ taliviyip pirintu vēṟṟu nāṭṭukkuc cellutalāl āya varuttattiṉkaṇṇum, taliviyip pirintu cellum tāṉ mēlum cellutali virumpātu mīṇṭu tirumpi varutalit taṉṉeñcattuṭaṉ ārāyum pakutikkaṇṇum, pirintu ceṉṟu viṉimuṟṟi mīṇṭuvantuḻip perukat tōṉṟiya ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum, perumpukaḻaminta ūrtiyākiya tēriṉic celuttum pākaṟkuk kūṟutaṟkaṇṇum, kāmakkiḻatti maṉivi eṉṉum ivarkaḷ pātukāvalākak kūṟiya kūṟṟiṟku etirmoḻi pakarumiṭattum, pirintu ceṉṟaviṭattut tāṉ uṟṟa varuttattiṉi mikavum viḷakkit taliviyiyiṉṟic ceṉṟa taṉatu taṉimiyi eṭutturikkumiṭattum, ariya ceyalic ceytu niṟivēṟṟiya taṉatu talimittaṉmi viḷaṅkiya kālattu viruntiṉaruṭaṉ nallaṉavaṟṟi virumpiya niliyilum, taṉṉi etirkoṇṭu varavēṟkum muṟiyil māli mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷi ēntiya maṉiviyarum makkaḷum cuṟṟattārum mēṟkoṇṭa ceyalmuṟikaḷi virumpiyēṟkum niliyilum, taṉṉoṭu paḻakum pāṇaṉ mutaliya ēṉiya vāyilkaḷāyiṉār kūṟum kūṟṟukku etirmoḻikūṟum kiḷaviyoṭu tokuttup paṇṇutalaminta kiḷavippakutikaḷ muppattu mūṉṟum eṇṇutaṟku ariya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya talimakaṉ kūṟutaṟkuriyaṉavām  e-ṟu.
iṅkukkūṟappaṭṭa muppattumūṉṟu pakutikaḷum eṇṇu taṟkariya peruñciṟappiṉiyuṭiya talivaṉ ceyalkaḷākavum kūṟṟukkaḷākavum varuvaṉellāvaṟṟiṟkum nilikkaḷamāka amintaṉa eṉpār paṇamipakuti muppatiṉorumūṉṟum eṇṇaruñciṟappiṉ kiḻavōṉmēṉa eṉṟār. muppatiṉoru mūṉṟu - muppattumūṉṟu,				          (5)

(koṭiyōr koṭumi cuṭumeṉa oṭiyātu nallici nayantōr colloṭu tokiip pakutiyiṉ nīṅkiya takutik kaṇṇum) koṭiyōr nallici nayantōr colloṭu tokii - koṭiyōreṉṟatu pāṇar kūttar viṟaliyar antaṇar mutaliyōri; koṭiyōrāyt talivaṉ pukaḻik kūṟutaṟku virumpiṉōr parattiyarkku vāyilāy vantu kūṟiya collōṭē tāṉum avariṭattē cērntu ; pakutiyi ṉīṅkiya koṭumi - kāvaṟpāṅkiṟ pakkamum āṅkōr pakkamumākiya pakuti kāttaliṉiṉṟu nīṅkiya parattiyarik kūṭiya koṭumi; cuṭumeṉa oṭiyātu takutikkaṇṇum - neñcic cuṭumeṉṟu kūṟi avaṉ tavaṟṟik kūṟutalit tavirāmaṟ kūṟutaṟkut takka takutiyiṭattum;
iṉ, nīkkapporuṭṭu; pakuti - kūṟupāṭu, ākupeyar; pakutikaḷik kāttaṟkup pirivēṉeṉak kūṟip pirintu pāṇar mutaliyōr putitiṟ kūṭṭiya parattiyariṭattē oḻukiya mey vēṟupāṭṭōṭu vantāṉik kaṇṭu appakutikaḷip parattiyarākak kūṟuvāḷāyiṟṟu. atu, iṇipaṭa nivanta eṉṉum marutak kaliyuḷ,

koṭumi oḻukkaṅ kōṭal4 vēṇṭi aṭimēl vīḻnta kiḻavaṉi neruṅkik5 kātal eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟeṉa mātarcāṉṟa vakiyiṉkaṇṇum: koṭumi oḻukkaṅ kōṭal vēṇṭi - aṅṅaṉam pakutiyiṉīṅkip parattiyarmāṭṭu oḻukik koṭumi ceyta oḻukkattit talivi poṟuttali vēṇṭi ; aṭimēl vīḻnta kiḻavaṉi neruṅki - taṉ aṭimēl vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiya talivaṉi ataṉiṉmītu tuṉimikkuk kaḻaṟi; kātal eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟeṉa - niṉmāṭṭuk kātaliyuṭiya eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ ivi naṉṟeṉak koḷvareṉak kūṟi; mātar cāṉṟa vakiyiṉkaṇṇum - kātal amintu māṟiya vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum:

avaṉvayiṟ pirippiṉum1 --- taṉṉoṭu mintaṉiṭi uṟavu nīkki avaṉit talivaṉoṭu cārttutaṟ kaṇṇum.

mipaṭaceṉṉi eṉṉum marutakkaliyuḷ,	(kali. 86)
		maṟiniṉṟu, tāmaṉṟa vantīt taṉar;
āyiḻāy, tāvāta eṟkut tavaṟuṇṭō kāvātīṅ
kītti yivaṉiyāṅ kōṭaṟkuc cīttiyāṅ
kaṉṟi ataṉik kaṭiyavuṅ kinnīvik
kuṉṟa viṟuvarik kōṇmā vivarntāṅkut
tanti viyaṉmārpiṟ pāyntāṉ aṟaṉillā
aṉpili peṟṟa makaṉ.
eṉpuḻi aṟaṉillā aṉpili peṟṟa makaṉ eṉavum, niṉ makaṉṟāyātal purivatā leṉavē eṉpuḻi niṉmakaṉ eṉavum pirittavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉṉāt tolcūḷ eṭuttaṟkaṇṇum1 - iṉṉāṅkup payakkuñ cūḷuṟaviṉit talivaṉ cūḷuṟuvaleṉak kūṟu miṭattum;
talivaṉ vantakuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukik, kaḷaviṟ cūḷuṟa vāṉ vanta ētam nīkki, ikkālattuk kaṭavuḷariyum putalvaṉiyuñ cūḷuṟutaliṉ iṉṉāta cūḷ eṉṟār. atu kaḷavupōlac cūḷuṟutaliṉ tolcūḷ eṉṟār.

kiḻavōḷ ceppal kiḻavatu eṉpa---ip pattoṉpatuṅ kiḻavōḷukku urimiyuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu. muṉṉar niṉṟa ēḻaṉurupukaḷit tokuttu iṉṉataṉ kaṇṇum iṉṉataṉkaṇṇun talivi cepputali vāyiliṉ vakiyōṭē kūṭṭik kiḻavōḷ ceppal vāyiliṉ varuum vakiyoṭu tokii eṉa māṟuka.	(6)
āyvuri :	itu, talivaṉi maṇantu vāḻum maṉi vāḻkkiyil talimakaḷ kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ)	talimakaṉatu niṉiviṉit talivi mikavum aṟivāḷātaliṉ avaṉi ārvamuṭaṉ varavēṟkumiṭattum. taṉṉi aṇukātavāṟu nīkki niṟuttumiṭattum, maṉiyaṟa vurimiyit tanta talivaṉiṭattu avaṉatu perumiyi matit toḻukalil tavaṟātaaṉpumītūrtaṟ kaṇṇum, talivaṉit talivi pirintu taṉimiyuṟṟa varuttam peritākaliṉ maṉac cuḻaṟci perukiya kāmattiṉ mikutikkaṇṇum. iṉpamum tuṉpamum taṉpāl oruṅkē tōṉṟiya niliyiṉum. putalvaṉ piṟanta niliyil nikaḻttap peṟum virumpattakka neyyāṭal viḻāviṟ kalantu koḷḷavirumpi vanta talivaṉi neñcu puṇpaṭumāṟu ceytu taṉṉoṭu kūṭutali vilakkiya tāḻvuṟṟa niliyilum, virumpiyavuḷḷattuṭaṉ putiya peṇṭiriṉ nalattiṉ poruṭṭup pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉit taṉatu taṉimittuyariṉi naṉku aṟivittu avaṉpāṟ celkiṉṟa taṉatu neñciṉi avaṉpāṟ cellutaliṉiṉṟum mīṭṭuc curuṅkac ceytu avaṉāṟ kātalikkap paṭṭāri etirilē tōṉṟa uruvakañ ceytu puṇarcciyi maṟutta aṉpiṉ pakkattum, parattiyarpāl taṅkiya puṟattoḻukkamuṭiya talivaṉi vaṇaṅki niṉatu vēṭkiyi eṉpāl uriyātu eṉ taṅkiyarāya parattiyarkku urippāyāka eṉa irantu vēṇṭutaṟkaṇṇum, tavivaṉ taṉṉi viṭṭu nīṅkānili yiṉaṉākiya kālattu (niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭum puṟappeṇṭirpāṟ) celvāyāka eṉa viṭuttaṟkaṇṇum, (talivaṉatu aṉpuk kuriyaḷākiya) kāmakkiḻatti taliviyākiya tāṉ peṟṟa kuḻantiyi aṉpuṭaṉ taḻuvi iṉpamuṟa viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ muṭivilum, ciṟanta aṉpiṉ ceyki pulappaṭum avviṭattut talivaṉ tōṉṟi maṉiyaṟatti virumpiya neñacattuṭaṉ tāṉ vanta ceyalit talivi yaṟiyātavāṟu puṟattē aṇintu niṉṟu talivipā luḷatākiya ūṭalip pōkka vēṇṭiya iṭattum, anniliyil tantiyarip paṇpiṉālum ceyaliṉālum oppar avartam mintar eṉa ulakam kūṟutalāl muṭivillāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya makavittantiyoṭu cārttip paḻitturikku miṭattum, koṭumiyuṭiyāratu koṭuntaṉmi avariyē piṉṉarccuṭum eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu taṉ tuṉpattiṉi yeṇṇit taḷarātu talivaratu naṟpukaḻi virumpiyavarkaḷatu collōṭu poruntik kaṇavaṉoṭu vēṟupaṭṭup piṇaṅkutaliṉiṉṟum nīṅkiya takutik kaṇṇum, taṉatu koṭiya puṟattoḻukkiṉip poṟuttukkōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṭimēl vīḻnta kaṇavaṉi iṭitturittu icceyal niṉpāṟ kātaluṭiya eṉ taṅkiyarāya parattiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟāmō eṉat taṉ kātal niṟivu tōṉṟak kūṟun tiṟattiṉ kaṇṇum, tāyari aṇuki avarkaḷāl nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyappeṟṟa taṉ makaṉip poymmiyoḻukkiṉaḷāya paratti niṉitta vaḻiyum, taṉṉiṭattut tōṉṟiya aṉpiṉ ciṟappiṉālum talivaṉpāl tōṉṟiya ayaṉmipaṟṟiya vēṟu pāṭṭiṉālum iṉṉāmiyit tarum niliyil talivaṉ ceyta paḻiya cūḷuṟavit taṉ maṉattukkoṇṭa niliyilum, talivaṉuṭiya kāmakkiḻattiyiṉ nalattiṉippārāṭṭat toṭaṅkit talivaṉ taṉṉi ayaṉmi ceytoḻukutalākiya tīmiyiṉkaṇ muṭittukkūṟum poruḷiṉkaṇṇum, koṭumi viḷikkum talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkiṉkaṇṇē tōḻi kūṟutaṟkuriya kūṟṟukkaḷiṉiṭiyē kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kaṟpiṉkaṇ tiriyātu vekuḷutalum pārāṭṭutalun tīmiyiṉ vēṟupaṭuttalum aṉpiṉāṟ pēṇik koḷḷutalum eṉa avviṭattu vēṟupaṭṭu varum palvēṟu nilimikkaṇṇum ēṉiya vāyilkaḷpāl varum kūṟṟuvakiyoṭu tokuttu ivviṭaṅkaḷil talivi kūṟṟunikaḻttal urimiyuṭiyatu eṉpar āciriyar  e---ṟu.

8.	tōḻiyuḷ ḷuṟutta vāyil pukuppiṉum
āvayiṉ nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḷampūraṇam :	eṉ eṉiṉ, ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ)	tōḻiyuḷḷiṭṭa vāyilkaḷip pōkaviṭṭa akkālattum1 muṟkūṟiya2 nikaḻumeṉ ṟurippar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.	(8)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu tōḻi mutaliya vāyilkaṭku eytāta teytuvittatu.

(i-ḷ.)	tōḻi uḷḷuṟutta vāyil pukuppiṉum3 talivaṉatu celavuk kuṟippu aṟintu avaṉic celavaḻuṅkuvit taṟkut tōḻi yuḷḷiṭṭa vāyilkaḷit talivi pōkaviṭṭa akkālattu avar mēlaṉa pōlak kūṟum kūṟṟukkaḷum; āvayiṉ nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar-talivi añciṉāṟpōla avvaccattiṉkaṇṇē nikaḻumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.	(e - ṟu.)
aṟaṉiṉṟi yayaltūṟṟum (kali.3) eṉṉum pālik kaliyuḷ iṟicciyum viṉiyumākiya pū mutaliyaṉa kūṟiyavāṟṟāl talivikkiraṅki nīr celavaḻuṅkumeṉak kūṟuvāḷ yāmirappavu memakoḷḷā yāyiṉi vṉapãṟatyilkaḷiyuṅTṭṭicrittavāṟufṇka.(8)
āŒîiu:	itu, talivaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉikkaṇ accam viḷippatu piṟitorunikaḻcciyum uṇṭeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ)	talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟāṉāka maṉikkaṇ irukkum talivi taṉatu āṟṟāmi karutit tōḻiyuḷḷiṭṭa vāyilkaḷit talivaṉpāl tūtākap pōkaviṭṭa kālattum avviṭattut talivaṉ ceyyum viṉikkaṇ accam nikaḻum eṉpar pulavar	e-ṟu.
pukuppiṉum eṉpuḻi ummi, muṟkūṟiyavāṟu aṉpuṟutakka talivi kiḷattalēyaṉṟi vāyilkaḷit talivaṉpāl tūtākap pōkaviṭṭakālattum eṉap poruḷtarutaliṉ iṟantatu taḻīiya eccavummiyākum. pukiṉum eṉṉātu pukuppiṉum, eṉṟamiyāl pukuttaṟku viṉimutal talivi eṉakkoḷka.

itaṉuḷ ivaḷ nutal tēmpumpaṭi nī tēṟṟiya colleṉavē cōrvukaṇṭu aḻittāḷeṉpatu uṇarntum ippoyccūḷ niṉakku eṉṉa payaṉit tarumeṉat tōḻi talivaṉi nōkkik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.

(periyōr oḻukkam peritu eṉak kiḷantu peṟutaki illāp piḻippiṉum) periyōr peṟutaki illāk kiḷattu --- naṉmakkaḷ peṟuntakimi illaṟamāyirukkumeṉṟuñ colli; periyōr oḻukkam periteṉak kiḷantu piḻippiṉum - naṉmakkaḷ oḻukum oḻukkam peritāyirukku meṉṟuñ collit tāṉ talivaṉi vaḻipāṭu tappiṉum : tōḻikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
periyōriyuṅ kiḷanteṉpataṉiyum iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭṭuka.1
veḷḷi viḻuttoṭi meṉkarup pulakki	(akam. 286)
itaṉuḷ aṟa ṉeṉṟatu illaṟatti; taṟṟakavuṭimi nōkki yeṉṟatu, taṉṉāl avvaṟaṉum poruḷun takutip pāṭuṭiyavān taṉmiyi nōkki eṉṟavāṟām; muṉṉiyatu eṉṟatu puṟattoḻukkatti; periyōroḻukka maṉiya veṉṟatu periyōr oḻukkam periya veṉṟavāṟu.
itu muṉṉar nikaḻnta poyccūḷpaṟṟi nummaṉōr māṭṭum iṉṉa poyccūḷ piṟakkumāyiṉ ivvulakattu meyccūḷ iṉi iṉṟām. ataṉāṟ periyōrit tamatu oḻukkattit tēruṅkāli ariyavāyiruntaṉaveṉat talivaṉi nōkkit tōḻi kūṟaliṉ avaṉi vaḻipāṭu tappiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. uḷḷuṟiyuvamam itaṟku ēṟkumāṟuṇarka.
avvayiṉ uṟutaki illāp pulaviyuṇ mūḻkiya kiḻavōḷ pāl niṉṟu keṭuttaṟkaṇṇum - talivaṉ aṅṅaṉam piṟaḻnta iṭattu avaṉ ceṉṟu cērun takimi illāmikkuk kāraṇamākiya pulaviyiṉkaṇ aḻuntiya talivipakkattāḷāy niṉṟu avaḷ pulaviyit tīrttaṟkaṇṇum :

vakipaṭa vanta kiḷavi1 ellām tōḻikku uriya eṉmaṉār pulavar. tōḻikūṟṟāyt talivikūṟṟiṉuḷ aṭaṅkuvataṉṟit tōḻikkē kūṟattakum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭākavanta kiḷavikaḷellān tōḻikku uriyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar (e - ṟu.)

āyvuri : itu, kaṟpiṉkaṇ tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum iṭaṅkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ)	peṟutaṟkariya periyaporuḷākiya tirumaṇam niṟivēṟiyapiṉ aminta piṟarāl vekuḷutaṟkariya maṉi vāḻkki marapu kāraṇattāl talivaṉuk kuḷatākiya ciṟappiṉip pārāṭṭiyurikkumiṭattum, kaḷavukkālattut tām uṟṟa iṭiyūṟukaḷ nīṅkiya tiṟattik kūṟumiṭattum, taṉ kaṭamikaḷiṟ ciṟitum cōrvillāta taliviyit talivaṉ virintu maṇantu koḷḷutal kuṟittut tāṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟu teyvam ataṉi muṭittamiyāl teyvattiṟkup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttal vēṇṭum eṉat talivaṉukkuk kūṟumiṭattum, pukaḻuṭiya perum poruḷākiya maṉivāḻkkiyiṉit taliviyiṉpāl vitta vaḻit talivaṉ taṉ kaṭamiyiṉi maṟantoḻukiyaviṭattum, puṟattoḻukkattiyuṭiya talivaṉ tiṟattu maṉam varunti ūṭiya taliviyi avaḷatu ūṭal taṇiya eṭuttuk kāṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya pakutiyiṭattum, parattamiyākiya kuṟṟattiṉip purintu vantirunta talivaṉi iṭittuk kūṟik kuṟṟantīrnta niliyil taliviyiṉ aṉpukkuriyavaṉāka ākkikkoṭukku miṭattum. paṇiviṉippulappaṭuttum moḻikaḷāl talivaṉi vaṇaṅkutaṟkaṇṇum, puṟattē ceṉṟu parattiyaroṭum viḷi yāṭutalip poruntiya talivaṉatu viruppattiṉkaṇṇum, maṉivāḻkkiyil aṉpukūrtaṟ ciṟappuṭiya makaṉit talivaṉ vāyilākak koṇṭu ceṉṟaviṭattut talivi vāyil nērāmiyāl talivaṉ taṉimiyuṟṟu varuntumiṭattum, talimakaḷiṭattiliruntu nī koṇṭa māṭcimi poruntiya nalattiṉi mīṭṭut tantu celvāyāka eṉat talivaṉi vēṟupaṭutturikkumiṭattum. talivaṉatu pēṇāvoḻukkiṉiyeṇṇi nāṇamuṟṟa niliyilum, talivaṉ tāṉ ceyta cūḷuṟaviṉiṉṟum piḻitta miyāl uḷavām tīmiyiṉiyeṇṇit talivi varuntiya niliyilum, periyōrāyiṉāratu oḻukalāṟu perumiyiṉ kaṇṇatākum eṉṟu kūṟit taliviyip peṟuntakiyillātavāṟu talivaṉ ceyta piḻiyiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟumiṭattum, mēṟcolliyavāṟu talivaṉ tavaṟu ceytuḻi avaṉāl aṇu kuntaṉmiyillāta pulaviyuḷ aḻuntiya taliviyiṉ cārpuṭiyaḷāy niṉṟu avaḷatu pulaviyi nīkkumiṭattum, talivaṉ taṉatu niliyiṉiyuṇartti ūṭal tīrka eṉa vēṇṭik koḷḷavum taṇiyāta ūṭalit tīrttal vēṇṭiya talivaṉatu cārpuṭiyaḷāyt taliviyi vekuṇṭu niṉṟu avaḷatu ūṭal tīrttu uṇṭākkiya maṉi vāḻkki yamitiyākiya takutiyiṭattum, kāṇutaṟku ariḷa kaḷavukkālattut tam perumiyip pulappaṭuttiya niliyil kaṟpuk kālattuttam eḷimiyik kūṟi iraṅkumiṭattum (taliviyatu ūṭal taṇivittal karuti) pāṇar, kūttar, viṟaliyar eṉṉum ivvāyilkaḷ virumpikkūṟiya kuṟiyirattalkaḷukku maṟumoḻi kūṟum niliyilum, parattamiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu taliviyik kiviṭṭa talivaṉit taliviyuṭaṉ maṉiyaṟamākiya naṉṉeṟiyil oḻukac ceytal vēṇṭit taliviyip pātukātta taṉmiyiṉālē kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟik kaṭinturittup parattamik kuṟṟattiṉiṉṟum talivaṉi mīṭṭaṟkaṇṇum, talivaṉ cēyiṭippiriviṟ (neṭuntūrappiriviṟ) pirintu cella eṇṇiya kālattu avaṉukku muṉṉiṉṟu kūṟiya---ulakiyaliṉ māṟupaṭāta etirmoḻi uṭpaṭa iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumākak kūṟupaṭa vanta kiḷavikaḷ ellām tōḻikku uriya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e---ṟu.

eṉ---eṉiṉ, kāmakkiḻattiyar kūṟṟu nikaḻum iṭam uṇarttiṟṟu.
iḷampūraṇam :	pullutaṉ mayakkum pulavi mutalākac collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉum annikaraṉa piṟaviṭattiṉum kuṟikkap paṭṭa kūṟṟuk kāmakkiḻattiyar mēlaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
kūṟṟeṉpatu atikārattāṉ vantatu.
kāmakkiḻattiyarāvār piṉmuṟi ākkiya kiḻattiyar. avar mūvakippaṭuvar; otta kiḻattiyarum iḻinta kiḻattiyarum variyappaṭṭārum eṉa. otta kiḻattiyar--- muntuṟṟa maṉiyāḷaṉṟik kāmam poruḷākap piṉṉun taṉ kulattuḷḷāḷ oruttiyi varital. iḻintārāvār---antaṇarkku aracakulattiṉum vaṇika kulattiṉum vēḷāṇ kulattiṉum koṭukkappaṭṭārum, aracarkku ēṉiyiraṇṭukulattiṉuṅ koṭukkappaṭṭārum. vaṇikarkku vēḷāṇkulattiṟ koṭukkappaṭṭārum variyap paṭṭār---celvarāyiṉār kaṇikik kulattiṉuḷḷārkkum iṟkiḻami koṭuttu varintu kōṭal. avar, kaṉṉiyil variyappaṭṭārum ataṉ piṉpu variyappaṭṭārum eṉa iru vakiyar. avviruvarum urimi pūṇṭamiyāṟ kāmakkiḻattiyarpāṟ paṭṭaṉar. parattiyarāvār yāreṉiṉ, avar āṭalum pāṭalum vallarāki aḻakumiḷamiyuṅ kāṭṭi iṉpamum poruḷum veḵki oruvar māṭṭun taṅkātār.1 ivaruḷḷum oruvarip paṟṟi maṟutalip peṇṭiric cārttik kūṟuvaṉavum kāmakkiḻattiyar kūṟṟiṉpāṟ paṭum. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu avaravar kūṟṟāṉaṟika. iccūttirattiṟ kāmakkiḻattiyeṉa ōtātu kiḻattiyar eṉa ōtutalāṉum palavakiyār eṉpatu koḷka.
pullutaṉ mayakkum2 pulavikkaṇṇum eṉpatu---pullutalik kalakkum pulavimāṭṭuṅ kāmakkiḻattiyar kūṟṟu nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵtāvatu mutirāta pulavimāttiramākiya puṇarcciyiyuṭaṉ paṭṭa neñcattaḷātal.

(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ tammoṭum piṟa makaḷiroṭum kūṭi makiḻtali viravi mēṟkoḷḷutalāl uḷatākiya pulaviyiṭattum, maṉiyakattārākiya talivi mutaliyōr tammi matiyātu ceytaceyalkaḷi ikaḻum niliyilum, talivaṉukkup peṇṭir palariṭattum piṟanta putalvarkaḷikkaṇṭu uvakimīkkūrnta niliyilum, kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukimaṇantu maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttum maṉivi tammikkuṟittuc ceyyum--- tamakkup poṟukka voṇṇātatāyp perukiya tuṉpattiṉ kaṇṇum. tampāluḷa tākiya kātaṟcōrviṉālum maṉiyaṟak kaṭamikaḷi āḷutalākiya oppuravuṭimiyiṉālum tāypōlniṉṟu iṭitturittumaṉikkiḻattiyākiya taliviyi vekuḷutaliṉṟit talivaṉoṭu porunti vāḻac ceytaṟkaṇṇum, tām vāyil maṟutta niliyil iṉiya makiḻcciyi viḷikkum putalvaṉi aṉpiṉāl taḻuvi aṇikalaṉaṇintu piṉṉar vāyilākak koṇṭu vanta vāyilkaḷiṭattum, maṉikkiḻattiyākiya taliviyōṭu ottavurimiyuṭiya vaḷākat taṉṉik karututalāl taṉṉiyotta ēṉimakaḷiriṉum tāṉ talivaṉukkuc ciṟattāḷ eṉak koṇṭa kōṭpāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum, talivaṉ nikaḻttuvaṉavākak kātalar kalantukoḷḷum viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ  eṉṟu collappaṭṭa ivaṟṟōṭu piṟavum maṉiviyuṭaṉ oppavittu eṇṇattakka kāmakkiḻattiyar kūṟṟu nikaḻttaṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷām. e-ṟu.
pullutal- puṇartal. mayakkutalāvatu orukāl tammiṭattum maṟṟorukāl piṟariṭattum āka viravi nikaḻacceytal.
maṉiyaṟakkaṭamikaḷil urimi kāmakkiḻattiyarkku uṇṭu eṉpatu pulappaṭak kaṇṇiya kāmakkiḻattiyar eṉa aṭipuṇart tōtiṉār. kaṇṇutal- karuppaṭutal: vāḻkkit tuṇiviyākiya maṉiviyoṭu oppa matikkap peṟutal.

		tītilēm eṉṟu teḷippavuṅ kinnīvi
yātoṉṟu meṅkaṇ maṟuttaravu illāyiṉ	(kali. 81)
eṉpatu ūṭal. piṟa iṭattum ūṭutal aṟintukoḷka.
		kalantanōy kimmikak kaṇpaṭā eṉvayiṉ
pulantāyum nīyāyiṟ poyyāṉē velkuvi	(kali. 46)
eṉpatu kuṟipiḻittuḻip pulantatu.
kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟiriyatu paṟitapu nāri	(kuṟun. 128)
eṉpataṉuḷ nāri teyvaṅ kākkum ayiri iriyi vēṭṭāṟpōl namakkariyaḷāyiṉāḷi nī vēṭṭā yeṉpataṉāṟ kuṟi piḻittuḻi ūṭiṉami kūṟiṟṟu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa uyttuṇarntu koḷka.	(15)
āyvuri : itu, talimakaṉukkuriyatōr iyal puṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ) talivaṉ taṉ tavaṟiṉmiyi uṇarttat talivi uṇarntu ūṭal nīṅkum elliyiṉik kaṭanta niliyiṉum. kaḷaviṉkaṇ talivi tāṉ ceyta kuṟiyit tappiya niliyiṉum maṉam vēṟupaṭṭuppiṇaṅkutalum appiṇakkam nīṭittalum talivaṉukku uriyaṉa  e---ṟu.
pulavi---ciṟitu poḻutu niṟkum piṇakkam.
ūṭal - neṭumpoḻutu nīṭittuḷatām piṇakkam.
uṇarppuvayiṉvārā ūṭal tōṉṟiṉ
pulatatal tāṉē kiḻavaṟkum variyār	(iṟiyaṉār---50)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal nūṟpā ittolkāppiyac cūttirattiṉi aṭiyoṟṟiyamintatākum.
16.	pulattalu mūṭalu mākiya viṭattuñ
colattaku kiḷavi tōḻik kuriya.
iḷampūraṇam :	itu tōḻikkuriya marapuṇarttiṟṟu.1
		alantāri yallaṉōy ceytaṟṟāṟ kāmam
pulantārip pullā viṭal	(kuṟaḷ. 1303)
itu kaṟpu.
		kalantanōy kimikak kaṇpaṭā vemvayiṟ
pulantāyu nīyākip poyyāṉē velkuvi,
yilaṅkutā ḻaruviyō ṭaṇikoṇṭa niṉmalic
cilampu pōṟ kūṟuva kūṟu
milaṅkē relvaḷi yivaḷuṭi nōyē.
itu kaḷavu.	(kalit. 16)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, muṉṉart, talivaṉ pulakku meṉṟār, avviṭattun tōḻiyē kūṟṟunikaḻttutaṟku uriyaḷeṉkiṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) pulattalum ūṭalum ākiya iṭattum - talivaṉ taliviyiyun tōḻiyiyum accuṟuttaṟkuc ceykiyākac ceytukoṇṭu pulattalum atunīṭṭittu ūṭalum uṭaṉ nikaḻttiyavaḻiyum ; colattaku kiḷavi tōḻikku uriya - collat takum paṇimoḻi2 tōḻikku uriya (e - ṟu).
eṉavē talivi kuṟippaṟintu tōḻi kūṟutalaṉṟit talivi tāṉē kūṟappeṟāḷeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē pāṭāṇṭiṇik kik kiḷiyāyiṉ talivi kūṟavumpeṟumeṉṟu koḷka.1 ummi ciṟappummi.
		tāyuyir vēṇṭāk kūruki ralavaṉ
naritiṉṟu parikkum ūra yāvatum
aṉpumutal uṟutta kātal 
iṉṟevaṉ peṟṟaṉi pintoṭi tiṟattē
alantāri	(kuṟaḷ. 1303)
eṉa varum.
ivi kaṟpil talivi kuṟippiṉāl tōḻi kūṟṟu vantaṉa.
pulantāyu nīyāyiṟ poyyāṉē velkuvi 	(kali. 46)
eṉṟu kaḷavil tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ talivi kuṟippiṉāl.
		kaṉipeyal naṭunāḷyāṉ kaṇmāṟak kuṟipeṟāaṉ
puṉiyiḻāy eṉpaḻi niṉakkurikkun tāṉeṉpa
tuḷinacii vēṭkiyāṉ micipāṭum puḷḷiltaṉ
aḷinaci ārvuṟṟa aṉpiṉēṉ yāṉāka	(kali. 46)
eṉat tōḻi colleṭuppataṟkut talivi ciṟupāṉmi kūṟutalum īṇṭu uriyaveṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
		yāṉūṭat tāṉuṇartta yāṉuṇarā viṭṭataṟpiṉ
tāṉūṭa yāṉuṇarttat tāṉuṇarāṉ---tēṉūṟuṅ
koytār vaḻutik kuḷircān taṇiyakalam
eytā tirākkaḻinta vāṟu.	(muttoḷḷāyiram, 104)
itaṉuḷ yāṉuṇarttat tāṉuṇarāṉeṉap pāṭāṇ tiṇik kikkiḷiyuḷ talivi kūṟiyatu kāṇka.	(16)
āyvuri :	itu tōḻikkuriyatō riyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ) talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvariṭiyē pulaviyum ūṭalum ākiya piṇakkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya niliyilum avaṟṟit taṇikkum vaṇṇam collutaṟkuriya kūṟṟukkaḷ tōḻikku uriyaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.
talivaṉ pulattalum atu nīṭṭitta niliyil ūṭalum koṇṭa niliyil talivi uriyāṭutal illiyeṉpatum, taliviyiṉ kuṟippaṟintu tōḻiyē uriyāṭutaṟku uriyaḷ eṉpatum innūṟpāvukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tarum viḷakkamākum.
17.	paratti maṟuttal vēṇṭiyuṅ kiḻavi
maṭattaku kiḻami uṭimi yāṉum
aṉpili koṭiyi eṉṟalum uriyaḷ.
iḷampūraṇam :	ituvum tōḻikku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttiṟṟu.
itu cūttirattāṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkum1
		makiḻcey tēmoḻit toyyilcūḻ iḷamuli
mukiḻceya muḷkiya toṭarpavaḷ uṇkaṇ
aviḻpaṉi uṟippavum nalkātu viṭuvāy
imiḻtirik koṇka koṭiyi kāṇnī;
ilaṅkēr elvaḷi ērtaḻi tii
nalañceya nalkiya toṭarpavaḷ cāayp
pulantaḻap pullātu viṭuvāy
ilaṅkunīrc cērppa koṭiyi kāṇnī.	(kalit. 125)
eṉa varum.	(17)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, collattakuṅ kiḷaviyē yaṉṟic collattakāk kiḷaviyun tōḻi kūṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) parattimi maṟuttal vēṇṭiyum - talivaṉ paṭiṟṟuḷḷattāṟ puṟattu oḻukum oḻukkattip pōkkutal virumpiyum; kiḻatti maṭattaku kiḻami uṭimiyāṉum--- talivi avaṉ parattimi aṟintēyum avaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi meyyeṉak koṇṭu cīṟṟaṅkoḷḷātu oḻukum maṭaṉeṉṉuṅ kuṇattiṟku ēṟṟaṉa aṟintoḻukum urimiyuṭiyaḷākiya eṇmiyāṉum: aṉpili koṭiyi eṉṟalum uriyaḷ--- talivaṉi aṉpili yeṉṟaluṅ koṭiyi yeṉṟalumuriyaḷ tōḻi (e - ṟu.)
koṭumi kaṭiyāyiṉār kuṇam. kaḷaviṉuḷ taṉ vayiṉurimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyuṅ kōṭaliṉ itaṟkup parattimi maṟuttal koḷka.
kaṇṭava rilleṉa ulakattuḷ uṇarātār	(kali. 125)
eṉṉum neytaṟkali kikōḷ iraṇṭiṟkuṅ koḷka.	(17)
āyvuri :	ituvum atu.
(i---ḷ.) parattiyari virumpiyoḻukum talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkatti nīkkutal karutiyum, talivi maṭaṉeṉṉum kuṇattāl aṭaṅkiyoḻukum eḷimiyuṭiyaḷātal karutiyum talivaṉi aṉpilikoṭiyi eṉat tōḻi iṭitturittaṟkum uriyaḷ e---ṟu.
paratti eṉṟatu, parattiyaric cērntoḻukalākiya puṟattoḻukkattic cuṭṭi niṉṟatu parattami eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. maṟuttal --- tīteṉavilakkutal maṭattaku kiḻamiyāvatu, talivaṉatu kuṟṟattiṉiyaṟintum aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu oḻukutalākiya maṭaṉ eṉṉuṅ kuṇattiṉit taṉakkuriya iyalpākakkoṇṭiruttal.
aṉpiliyi koṭiyi eṉṟal tōḻiyāṟ collat takāta kiḷaviyāyiṉum. talivaṉatu parattamiyoḻukkatti iṭitturittut tavirttal kāraṇamākavum, avaṉatu kuṟṟatti nēril aṟintum vekuṇṭu vilakka muṭiyāta maṭamikkuṇattiṉaḷ taliviyātalāṉum iṅṅaṉam kaṭuñcoṟkūṟit talivaṉit tiruttuvataṟku uriyāḷ tōḻi eṉṟār āciriyar.
18.	avaṉkuṟip paṟital vēṇṭiyuḷ kiḻavi
akamali yūṭal akaṟcik kaṇṇum
vēṟṟumik kiḷavi tōṟṟavum peṟumē.
iḷampūraṇam :	itu talivik kuriyatoru marapuṇart tiṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ kuṟippaṟitalvēṇṭiyun talivi taṉatu akamalinta ūṭal nīṅkum iṭattiṉum vēṟṟumikkiḷavi1 tōṟṟavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
yārivaṉ eṉ kūntal koḷvāṉ	(kalit. 89)
eṉavum,
yāriyō emmil pukutaruvāy	(kalit 68)
eṉavum kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.	(18)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, talivikku āvatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṉ kuṟippu aṟital vēṇṭiyum - tōḻi aṉpili koṭiyiyeṉak kēṭṭa talivaṉ muṉinta uḷḷattaṉāṅkollōveṉa aiyuṟṟu avaṉatu kuṟippi aṟital vēṇṭiyum; akaṉmali ūṭal akaṟcik kaṇṇum2 - taṉatu neñcil niṟintuniṉṟa ūṭal kiyikantu tuṉiyākiya vaḻi iḵtu avaṟku evaṉāṅkolleṉa añciya vaḻiyum ; kiḻavi vēṟṟumik kiḷavi tōṟṟavum peṟum - talivi talivaṉoṭu ayaṉmiyuṭiya collit tōṟṟuvikkavum peṟum (e-ṟu.)
		naṉṉalan toliya nalamikac cāay
iṉṉuyir kaḻiyiṉum uriyal avarnamak
kaṉṉiyum attaṉum allarō
pulaviya tevaṉō aṉpilaṅ kaṭiyē.	(kuṟun. 93)
itaṉuḷ avari aṉpili koṭiyiyeṉṉāti, aṉpil vaḻi niṉpulavi avari eṉceyyum avar namakku iṉṟiyamiyāta emarallarōveṉa iruvakiyāṉum ayaṉmi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.	(18)
āyvuri :	itu, talivikkuriyatōr iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉatu uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi aṟintu koḷḷutalivirumpiyaviṭattum taṉatu neñcam aṉpiṉāl ūṭal nīṅkumiṭattum uṟaviṉmiyip pulappaṭuttuñcol talimakaḷ pāl tōṟṟavum peṟuma e-ṟu.
akammali ūṭal akaṟci --- taṉmaṉattēyuṇṭākiya makiḻcci kāraṇamāka ūṭal akalutal. akalutal --- nīṅkutal.
vēṟṟumikkiḷaviyāvatu. oṉṟiya kēṇmiyiṉarā yoḻukum kātalar iruvariṭiyē ivar namakku ayalār eṉa eṇṇum vēṟupāṭṭuṇarviṉit tōṟṟuvikkum col.
19.	kāmak kaṭappiṉuṭ paṇinta kiḷavi
kāṇuṅ kālik kiḻavōṟ kurittē
vaḻipaṭu kiḻami avaṭkiya lāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam :	itu, talimakaṟ kuriyatoru marapuṇarttiṟṟu.
itu, cūttirattāṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkum. 1 
orūu, koṭiyiyal nallār kural nāṟṟattuṟṟa
 eṉṉum marutakkaliyuḷ,
periyārk kaṭiyarō āṟṟātavar 
eṉat talivi kūṟiyavaḻi,
kaṭiyar tamak kiyār colat takkār maṟṟu (kalit. 88) 
eṉavarum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	itu, talivi vēṟṟumikkiḷavi tōṟṟiya piṉṉart talivaṟku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kāmak kaṭappiṉuṭ paṇinta kiḷavi 1 -aṅṅaṉan talivikaṇṇun tōḻikaṇṇum vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭuḻit taṉakkuk kāmaṅ kiyikantuḻit tāḻntukūṟuṅ kūṟṟu: kāṇuṅ kāli kiḻavōṟku urittē-ārāyuṅ kālattut talivaṟku urittu; vaḻipaṭu kiḻami avaṭku iyalāṉa- avaṉi eññāṉṟum vaḻipaṭṭoḻukutal talivikku illaṟattōṭu paṭṭa iyal pākalāṉ (e-ṟu.)
		āyiḻāy, niṉkaṇ peṟiṉallāl iṉṉuyir vāḻkallā eṉka ṇevaṉō tavaṟu	(kali. 88)
kaṭiyartamak, kiyārcollat takkār māṟṟu	(kali.88)
niṉṉāṇi kaṭakkiṟpā riyār	(kali.81)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa koḷka.
kāṇuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāl talivaṉ taliviyetir pulappatu taṉṟavaṟu ciṟitākiya iṭatteṉavum, iṅṅaṉam paṇivatu taṉ tavaṟu peritākiya iṭatteṉavuṅ koḷka. 2 	(19)
	āyvuri:	itu, talivarkkuriyatōr iyal puṇarttu kiṉṟatu. 
(i-ḷ.) kāmavuṇarvumikkut tōṉṟiya niliyiṟ paṇintu kūṟuṅkūṟṟu  ārāyuṅkāla kaṇavaṉukkuriyatākum; kaṇavaṉi ekkālattum vaḻipaṭṭoḻukum urimi maṉivikkuriya ciṟappiyalpu ākalāṉ  e-ṟu.
kāmakkaṭappu eṉṟatu, kāmavaṇarvu taṉakkuriya elliyik kaṭantu mikka niliyiṉi, paṇinta kiḷavi-paṇiviṉip pulappaṭuttum muṟiyil vaṇaṅkik kūṟum col. kāṇutal-maṉattāṟ karuti yuṇartal. vaḻipaṭu kiḻami avaṭku iyavāṉa,paṇinta kiḷavi kiḻavōṟku urittu eṉa iyiyum.
20.	aruṇmun tuṟutta aṉpupoti kiḷavi
poruḷpaṭa moḻital kiḻavōṭ kurittē
iḷampūraṇam:	itu, talimakaṭkuriyatōr iyalpuṇarttiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ ) 1 poruḷpaṭa moḻitalāvatu poyyāk kūṟātu meyyē kūṟal.
utāraṇam vanta vaḻik kāṇka.	(20)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	itu, talivaṉ paṇintu moḻin tāṅkut taliviyum paṇintu kūṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ.) aruḷ muntuṟutta aṉpu poti kiḷavi 2 piṟar avalaṅ kaṇṭu avalikkum aruḷ muṉ tōṟṟuvitta avvaruḷ piṟattaṟku ētuvāki eññāṉṟum akattu nikaḻu aṉpiṉik  karantu colluṅ kiḷavi: poruḷpaṭamoḻital kiḻavōṭkum urittē-paṇintamoḻi tōṟṟātu vēṟōr poruḷ payappak kūṟutal talivikkum urittu (e-ṟu.)
vēṟu poruḷāvatu talivaṉ kūṟiyāṅkut tāṉum paṇintu kūṟuvāḷ,paṇiyātē taṉ neñcu taṉṉiyuṅ ki kaṭantu avaṉ ēvalic ceytateṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutalumām.
itu taṉvayiṟ karattalum avaṉvayiṉ vēṭṭalum (tol.po 11 ) eṉap poruḷiyaluḷ vaḻuvamittaṟku ilakkaṇam.
iṇiyiraṇṭu eṉṉum marutatkaliyuḷ,
		mācaṟa maṇṇuṟṟa maṇiyēcu miruṅkūntul
vicērntu vaṇṭārkkuṅ kaviṉpeṟal vēṇṭēṉ
nōy cērnta tiṟampaṇṇi niṉpāṇaṉ ummaṉi
nīcērnta vilviṉāy vārāmaṟ peṟukaṟpiṉ	(kali. 77)
eṉak kūṟiya talivi.
		kaṭiiya niṉmārpu tōyalam eṉṉum
iṭiyu niṟiyum eḷitōniṟ kāṇiṉ
kaṭavupu kittaṅkā neñceṉṉun tammō
ṭuṭaṉvāḻ pakiyuṭi yārkku	(kali. 77)
eṉpuḻi niṟkāṇiṟ kaṭavupu kittaṅkā neñceṉavē avaṉ āṟṟāmi kaṇṭaruḷi neñcu ēvalceytateṉa vēṟōr poruḷ payappak kūṟit taṉ aṉpiṉik karantavāṟu kāṇka.
kūrmuṇ muḷḷi (akam. 26) eṉpataṉuṭ ciṟupuṟaṅ kaviyiṉaṉ eṉa avaṉ varuntiyatu ētuvākat tāṉ maṇpōṉ ñekiḻntēṉēṉa aruṇ muntuṟuttavāṟum, ivi pārāṭṭiya paruvamum uḷaveṉa aṉpupotintu kūṟiyavāṟum, āṇṭum paṇintamoḻi veḷippaṭāmal neñcaṟipōkiya aṟiviṉēṟkeṉat taṉ aṟiviṉi vēṟākki ataṉmēliṭṭuk kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
āyvuri :	itu, talivikku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aruṭpaṇpiṉit tōṟṟuvikkum aṉpiṉi yuḷḷaṭakkiya coṟkaḷip paṇitaṟporuḷ tōṉṟac collutal talivikkum uriyatu e-ṟu.
aṉpiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟum muṟiyil amintatu. aruḷ muntuṟutta  aṉpu eṉṉum tolkāppiyat toṭarākum. itaṉi aṭiyoṟṟi aruḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkūṟuvatāka amintatu. aruḷeṉṉum aṉpīṉkuḻavi (757) eṉavarum tirukkuṟaḷ toṭarākum.
poruḷ eṉṟatu, mēlic cūttirattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa paṇinta moḻipporuḷi, paṭutal-tōṉṟutal. kiḻavōṭkum eṉapuḻi ummi kiḻavōṟkē yaṉṟik kiḻavōṭkum eṉa iṟantatutaḻīiya eccavummiyākum.
21.	kaḷavuṅ kaṟpum alarvari viṉṟē.
iḷampūraṇam:	eṉ-eṉiṉ. alar āmāṟu uṇarttiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.)kaḷaviṉuḷ kaṟpiṉum alarāku meṉṟu kūṟutal variviṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu. 1
tokuttuk kūṟal eṉpataṉāṟ kaḷavum kaṇṭu otuppaṭṭatu.
		kaṇṭatu maṉṉum orunāḷ avarmaṉṉun
tiṅkaḷip pāmpukoṇ ṭaṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ. 1146)
itu kaḷavu. 
		vētiṉa veriniṉ ōti mutupōt
tāṟucel mākkaḷ puṭkoḷap poruntuñ 
curaṉē ceṉṟaṉar kātalar uraṉaḻin
tīṅkiyāṉ aḻuṅkiya vvvamaṅkaṟinjṉṟivtḻuṅkaÿrē.(kuWª 310)
itu kaṟpu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:-	ituvuṅ talivikkut tōḻikkum uriya kūṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷaviṉkaṇṇum kaṟpiṉkaṇṇum alareḻu kiṉṟateṉṟu kūṟutal talivikkun tōḻikkum nīkkunilimiyiṉṟu (e-ṟu). 
variviṉṟeṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamiyāṉ iruvariyuṅ koṇṭām. talivaṉ āṅkuk kūṟuvaṉāyiṟ kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭamiṉmiyuṅ kaṟpiṟ piriviṉmiyum piṟakkum. oppakkūṟa   leṉṉum utti paṟṟik kaḷavum uṭaṉōtiṉār cūttirañ  curuṅkutaṟku. kaḷavalarāyiṉum (tol. kaḷa. 24) eṉavum ampalu malarum (tol.kaḷa.48) eṉavuṅ kaḷaviṟ kūṟiyavi alarāy nikaḻntavaḻi vēṟucila poruṇmi paṟṟik kūṟutaṟku vantaṉa. avi alarkūṟap peṟupa eṉṟaṟku vantaṉa vaṉṟeṉa uṇarka.1
		karumpiṉ entiraṅ kaḷiṟṟetir piḷiṟun
tērvaṇ kōmāṉ tēṉūr aṉṉivaḷ
nallaṇi nayantunī tuṟattaliṟ
pallō raṟiyap pacantaṉṟu nutalē.	(aiṅkuṟu.55)
itu, tōḻi alar kūṟiyatu.	(21)
āyvuri:	itu, kaḷaviṉum kaṟpiṉum talivikkum tōḻikkum uriyatōr tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) muṟkūṟiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉum kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa kaṟpiyaloḻukkattiṉum alar eḻukiṉṟatu eṉakkūṟutal nīkkum nilamiyiṉṟu. e-ṟu.
alar-alartal; virital: eṉṟatu, kātalar iruvariṭiyē  maṟivil nikaḻum naṭpu ūrār palarkkum pulaṉāka makaḷiriṭiyē veḷippaṭṭuc col nikaḻtal, alar eṉpatu iṉṉāṉōṭu iṉṉāḷiṭiyatu pōlum paṭṭatu eṉaccolli niṟpatu eṉpar iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviya luriyāciriyar. talivaṉatu oḻukavāṟupaṟṟi ōrmakaḷiriṭiyē alar paravutaliyaṟintu añci atupaṟṟit talivaṉpāṟ pulappaṭuttaṟku uriyavarkaḷ tōḻiyum taliviyum ātaliṉ innūṟpā avviruvarkkumuriya tiṟam kūṟiyatu eṉak karutturikkappaṭṭatu.
22.	alaril tōṉṟuṅ kāmattu mikuti.1 
iḷampūraṇam: 
		ūravar keḷavi eruvāka aṉṉicol
nīrāka nīḷumin nōy	(kuṟaḷ 1147)
eṉavum varum.	(22)
	alaril tōṉṟuṅ kāmattiṟ ciṟappē.2 
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	 iḵtu alar kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ iḵtu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) alariltōṉṟuṅ kāmattumikuti-iruvakik kikōḷiṉum piṟanta alarāl3 talivaṟkun talivikkuṅ kāmattiṭattu mikuti4 tōṉṟum (e-ṟu).
eṉṟatu, kaḷavu alarākiyavaḻi iṭiyīṭṭiṟku añciya accattāṉ iruvarkkuṅ kāmañciṟattaluṅ kaṟpiṉuṭ parattimi yāṉ alartōṉṟiyavaḻik kāmañciṟattalum avaḷ varuntumeṉṟu talivaṟkuk kāmañciṟattalun talivaṉ piriviṉkaṭ ṭalivikkuk kāmañ ciṟattalum piṟavumām.
ūravar keḷavi	(kuṟaḷ.1147)
		neyyā lerinutuppē meṉṟaṟṟāṟ keḷaviyāṟ 
kāma nutuppē meṉal.	(kuṟaḷ 1148) 
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa koḷka.	(u)
āyvuri:	iḵtu alarāl viḷivatu itu veṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) alarmoḻiyāl talivaṉ talivi iruvaruḷḷattum kāmavēṭki mikkuttōṉṟum e-ṟu.
alariṉ alarmoḻiyiṉāl: iṉṉurupu ētupporuḷil vantatu. alariṉ kāmattu mikuti tōṉṟum eṉa iyittup poruḷ koḷka.
23.	kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭāṅkum aṟṟē.
iḷampūraṇam: ituvum atu
(i-ḷ) kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭṭum kāmattiṉ mikutiyik kāṭṭum eṉṟavāṟu.1
āṅku-aci.

eṉavarum.
viḷiyāṭṭāṟ kāmamikku uṟaṅkāmi kaṇṭukoḷka. (23)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:	ituvuṅ kāmacciṟappē kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭṭu 2 -talivaṉ parattiyar cēriyuḷ āṭalum pāṭalum kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum avaruṭaṉyāṟu mutaliyaṉa āṭiyum iṉpam nukarum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum, āṅkum 3 aṟṟu 4 ---ap parattiyariṭattum alarāl tōṉṟuṅ kāmac ciṟappu (e-ṟu.)
āṅkum eṉṟa ummiyāṉ īṅkum1  aṟṟeṉak koḷka. tammoṭu talivaṉ āṭiyatu palaraṟiyātavaḻi yeṉṟumām. palaraṟinta vaḻi avaṉatu pirivu tamakku iḻiveṉappaṭutaliṉ avar kāmacciṟappuṭiyarām. talivaṉ avaroṭu viḷiyāṭi alar kēṭkuntōṟun talivikkup pulattalum ūṭalum piṟantu kāmac ciṟappeytum. āṅkum īṅkumeṉavē avviruvariṭattun talivaṉ avi nikaḻttiṉāṉākaliṉ avaṟkuṅ kāmac ciṟappu oruvāṟṟāṟ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. itu kāmakkiḻattiyarallāta parattiyaroṭu viḷiyāṭiya pakutiyākaliṉ vēṟu kūṟiṉār. kāmakkiḻattiyar ūṭalum viḷiyāṭalun talivi ūṭalum viḷiyāṭalum yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉum (tol. po. 191) eṉpuḻik kūṟupa aḵtu alareṉappaṭāmiyiṉ2 viḷiyāṭṭukkaṇ ṇeṉa viritta urupu viṉiceyyiṭattu vantatu.3
		eḵkuṭi eḻiṉalat toruttiyoṭu neruni
vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi yeṉpa atuvē
poypuṟam potintayāṉ karappavuṅ kiyikan
talarā kiṉṟāl tāṉē	(akam. 116)
eṉavum.
kōṭutōy malirniṟi āṭi yōrē	(akam. 166)
eṉat taliviyum parattiyum piṟar alarkūṟiyavaḻik kāmañ ciṟantu pulantavāṟu kāṇka. āṇṭup paṇintu kūṟuṅkālum viḷiyāṭuṅkālun talivaṉ kāmac ciṟappuk kāṇka,	(23.)
āyvuri :	ituvum kāmavēṭki mikutaṟkuriya iṭam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ) talivaṉatu viḷiyāṭṭu nikaḻcciyākiya anniliyum avarpōṉṟu kāmavēṭkiyi mikutippaṭuttum avviyal piṉiyuṭiyatām e---ṟu.
viḷiyāṭṭu āṅku---viḷiyāṭṭu nikaḻcciyākiya avviṭam. aṟṟu---attaṉmittu.
24.	maṉivi talittāṭ1 kiḻavōṉ koṭumi
tammuḷa vātal vāyilkaṭ killi.
iḷampūraṇam :	eṉ eṉiṉ, vāyilkaṭ kuriyatoru mara puṇarttiṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) maṉivimāṭṭuk kiḻavōṉ koṭumiyit tāṅkār kūṟal vāyilkaṭkilli eṉṟavāṟu.	(24)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, vāyilkaṭku uriya ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) maṉivi tāṭṭali talivi ettiṟattāṉum pulantuḻi avaḷiṭattu; kiḻavōṉ koṭumi - talivaṉ koṭuntoḻilkaḷi; tam uḷa ātal - tam urikkaṇ uḷavākki urittal; vāyilkaṭku illi - tōḻi mutaliya vāyilkaḷuk killi (e - ṟu).
tāṭṭaliyeṉa māṟuka. atu pātattiṭatteṉṉun takutic col. atu vāyilkaḷ kūṟṟāy vantatu.2 utāraṇam vantuḻik kāṇka.	(24)
āyvuri :	itu, vāyilkaṭku uriyatōr tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) maṉiviyiṉ muṉṉiliyil kaṇavaṉatu puṟat toḻukkamākiya koṭumiyiṉit tam uriyāṭaliṟ koṇṭu kūṟutal vāyilkaṭku illi e---ṟu.
vāyilkaḷāvār. kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum ākiya kātalar iruvaratu karuttiṉiyum uḷattiṟ koṇṭu avviruvarum aṉpiṉāl oṉṟi nikaḻttum maṉiyaṟattiṟkut tuṇi niṉṟutavum pārppāṉ, pāṇaṉ. pāṭiṉi, iḷiyar, viruntiṉar, kūttar mutaliyōr. kaṇavaṉ maṉivi eṉṉum iruvarum oṉṟiya kēṇmiyiṉarāy maṉi vāḻkkiyiṉi māṇpuṟa nikaḻttutaṟkut tuṇi niṉṟu utavum kaṭamiyiṉarākiya vāyilkaḷē kaṇavaṉatu koṭumiyiṉi maṉivikkup pulaṉāka avaḷatu muṉṉiliyiṟ kūṟuvarāyiṉ avar tam kūṟṟiṉāl maṉiviyiṉ maṉam varunta maṉivāḻkki māṭcimi peṟa iṉitu nikaḻātu. ātalāl maṉiviyiṉ muṉṉark kaṇavaṉatu koṭumiyiṉi vāyilkaḷ tammuṭ koṇṭu kūṟutal kūṭātu eṉa aṟivuṟuttum muṟiyil amintatu innūṟpāvākum.
25,	maṉivi muṉṉark kiyaṟu kiḷavi1
maṉivik kuṟuti uḷvaḻi uṇṭē
iḷampūraṇam :	eṉ-eṉiṉ, ituvumatu.
(i---ḷ) maṉivi muṉṉarc ceyalaṟṟuk kūṟuñ col maṉivikku uṟutiyuḷvaḻi vāyilkaṭku uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu.
		iṉiyavar, variṉum nōymarun tallāy vārā
tavaṇar ākuka kātalar ivaṇ nam
kāmam paṭarpaṭa varuttiya
nōymali varuttaṅ kāṇaṉmā ravarē.	(25)
eṉa varum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttatu; iṉṉuḻi yāyiṟ peṟumeṉṟaliṉ.
(i---ḷ) maṉivi muṉṉark kiyaṟu kiḷavi - talivi muṉṉart talivaṉ kāmakkaṭappiṉāṟ paṇiyun tuṇiyaṉṟi nammik kiyikantāṉeṉak kiyaṟṟuk kūṟuṅkūṟṟu: maṉivikku uṟuti uḷvaḻi uṇṭē---pulantuvaruna talivikku maruntāy avaṉ kūṭuvatōr āṟṟal uṟuti payakkumāyiṉ1 avvāyilkaṭku uḷatām (u---ṟu.)
aṟiyā miyiṉ aṉṉi yañci	(naṟṟiṇi. 50)
itaṉuḷ, eṉṉaṟiyāmiyālē aṉṉāy niṉṉiyañci yāṅ kaḷvaṉ tuṇaṅkiyāṭuṅ kaḷavik kiyakap paṭuppēmākac cellāniṟka, avaṉ kuḻi mutaliyavaṟṟi uṭiyaṉāyt teruvu muṭinta iṭattē etirppaṭṭāṉāka, avvaruḷāmiyiṉ yāṇṭiyatu eṉkaṭ pacaliyeṉṟāṉāka, avaṉetirē eṉciṟumi peritākalāṉ ārāyātē tuṇintu nāṇili eluva vantēṉeṉat tōḻi meyyāṉum poyyāṉum puṉin turittavāṟu kāṇka. ēṉiya vāyilkaḷ kūṟṟuvantuḻik kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉan talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟuvaṉa aṉputalip pirinta kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ (tol, po. 179) eṉpuḻik kūṟutum.
āyvuri:	itu, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) talivaṉatu koṭumi kāraṇamāka maṉiviyiṉ muṉṉiliyil vāyilkaḷāvār ceyalaṟṟukkūṟuñcol maṉiviyiṉ āṟṟāminōykku maruntāy uṟuti viḷikkumāyiṉ avvāyil kaṭku uṇṭu. e---ṟu.
kiyaṟukiḷavi, ceyalaṟṟa taṉmiyip pulappaṭuttum muṟiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum iraṅkalmoḻi.
eṉavē, maṉivikku uṟutiviḷittal iṉṟi mēlum irakkattiṉi mikuvikkumāyiṉ kiyaṟukiḷavi vilakkappaṭum eṉpatām.
26.	muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi ellā vāyiṟkum
piṉṉilit tōṉṟum eṉmaṉārpulavar.
iḷampūraṇam :	ituvumatu.
(i - ḷ.) muṉṉilip puṟamoḻiyākak kūṟuñ col ellā vāyilkaṭkum uriya; piṉṉilimuyaluṅkāl tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
muṉṉilip puṟamoḻiyāvatu muṉṉiliyāka niṟpārik kuṟittup piṟaṉik kūṟumāṟupōlak kūṟutal.
		uṇkaṭaṉ vaḻimoḻin tirakkuṅkāl mukaṉuntāṅ
koṇṭatu koṭukkuṅkāl mukaṉumvē ṟākutal
paṇṭumiv vulakat tiyaṟki	(kalit. 22)
eṉṟavaḻi avvuri talimakaṉi nōkkiyavāṟu kāṇka.   (26)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu vāyilkaṭku uriyatōr pakuti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi - muṉṉiliyāy niṟkiṉṟa talivaṉi nōkkip piṟarik kūṟumāṟupōlak kūṟutal; ellā vāyiṟkum-paṉṉiraṇṭu vāyilkaḷukkum; piṉṉilit tōṉṟum eṉmaṉār pulavar - kuṟivēṇṭi muyaluṅkāl tōṉṟumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar (e - ṟu).
		uṇkaṭaṉ vaḻimoḻin tirakkuṅkāl ...
tāyuyir peyta pāvi pōla
nalaṉuṭi yārmoḻikkaṇ tāvārtān tannalan
tātutēr paṟaviyiṉaruntiṟal koṭukkuṅkāl
ētilār kūṟuva tevaṉōniṉ poruḷ vēṭki
	(kali. 22)
eṉat talivaṉi nōkki muṉṉilip puṟamoḻiyākak1 kūṟiṟṟu.
āyvuri ituvum vāyilkaṭ kāvatōr coṟṟiṟam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉṉiliyil uḷḷārik kuṟittup paṭarkkiyiṭa mākiya paṟattēyuḷḷār pōṟ kūṟum col, ellā vāyil kaṭkum kuṟivēṇṭip piṉṉiṟkuṅkāl tōṉṟum eṉpār aṟiñar e - ṟu.
piṉṉili - kuṟivēṇṭip piṉṉiṟṟal.
27.	tollavi urittalum nukarcci ēttalum
pallāṟ ṟāṉum ūṭalil takittalum1
uṟuti kāṭṭalum aṟivumeyn niṟuttalum
ētuviṉ urittalum tuṇiyak kāṭṭalum*
aṇinili urittalum kūttar mēṉa.
iḷam pūraṇam :-	eṉ eṉiṉ, kūttarkkuriya tiṟaṅ kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) tollavi yuritta lāvatu---muṉpuḷḷār ivvāṟu ceyvareṉak kūṟutal,
nukarcci yētta lāvatu---nukarcci yivvāṟu iṉiya toṉṟeṉap pukaḻtal
pallāṟṟāṉum ūṭaliṟ ṟakittalum eṉpatu---pala neṟiyāṉum ūṭaliṉiṉṟun talimakaḷi mīṭṭal: aḵtāvatu ivvāṟu ceytal kuṟṟameṉṟāṉum aṉpuṭiyār ceyyāreṉ ṟāṉum2 maṉikkiḻattiyar ceyalaṉṟu eṉṟāṉum ivvāṟu kūṟutal.
uṟutikāṭṭa lāvatu---ivvūṭal taṇintataṉāṟ payaṉitu veṉavum naṉmi payakkum eṉavum kūṟutal.
aṟivu meynniṟuttalāvatu---talimakaḷ meyyiṉkaṇ mikka tuṇiviṉāṟ  keṭṭavaṟivi itu takkataṉṟeṉa aṟivu koḷuttutal.
ētuviṉurittalum eṉpatu --- ivvāṟu ceyyiṉ ivvāṟu kuṟṟam payakkum eṉa ētuviṉāṟ kūṟutal. atu piṟaḷ orutti keṭṭapaṭi kūṟutal.
tuṇiyakkāṭṭa lāvatu---avaḷ tuṇiyumāṟu kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutal.
aṇinili yurittalāvatu---ivvāṟu uḷatākiya aṇiyip pularaviṭukiṉṟataṉāṟ payaṉ eṉṉi yeṉak kūṟutal.
ivi yellām kūttar mēlaṉa eṉṟavāṟu. avar ellā neṟiyiṉāṉum puṉin turikka valla rātalāṉ avar mēlaṉa eṉa urittār. ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. innūl vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum paṟṟi nikaḻtaliṉ ipporuṇmēl varum vaḻakkuri utāraṇamām1	(27)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, kūntarkkuriya kiḷavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i --- ḷ) tollavi urittalum - muṉpē mikkār iruvar iṉpam nukarntavāṟu ituveṉak kūṟalum; nukarcci ēṟṟalum - numatu nukarcci avariṉuñ ciṟantateṉak kūṟalum; pal āṟṟāṉum ūṭalil taṇittalum - illaṟak kiḻamikku iyalpaṉṟeṉṟāyiṉuṅ iḵtu aṉpiṉmiyā meṉṟāyiṉuṅ kūṟit taliviyi ūṭaliṉiṉṟu mīṭṭalum; uṟutikāṭṭalum - ilvāḻkki nikaḻtti iṉpanukartalē niṉakkup poruḷeṉṟalum;
iṉik kūṟuvaṉa2 talivaṟkuriya : aṟivu mey niṟuttalum. puṟattoḻukkam mikka talivaṟku nī kaṟṟaṟinta aṟivu iṉi meyyāka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉi meyyaṟiviṉ kaṇṇē niṟuttalum; ētuviṉ uṇarttalum - ik kaḻikāmattāṉ iḻivu talivaru meṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟalum; tuṇivu kāṭṭalum- ataṟkēṟpak kaḻikāmattāṟ keṭṭāri eṭuttukkāṭṭalum; aṇinili urittalum - muliyiṉun tōḷiṉum mukattiṉum eḻutuṅkāṟ puṇarccitōṟum aḻitteḻutumāṟu ituveṉak kūṟalum; kūttar mēṉa - ivveṭṭuṅ kūttariṭattaṉa (e---ṟu)
kūttar, nāṭakacāliyar, toṉṟupaṭṭa naṉṟun tītuṅ kaṟṟaṟintavaṟṟi avikkellām aṟiyakkāṭṭutaṟku uriyarākaliṟ kūttar iviyum kūṟupaveṉṟār ilakkiyam ik kālattiṟantaṉa.
iḵtu aṟivumeynniṟuttatu.
āyvuri :	itu, kūttarkkuriya tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟatu,

eṉpatu koḷka kavavoṭu mayaṅkiya kāli eṉpataṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka.	(32)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, talivi pulavi kaṭik koḷḷum 1 kālam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i - ḷ.) tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīik kōṭal - parattiyiṟ pirivu nīṅkiya talivaṉ taṉṉiṉum uyarnta kuṇattiṉaḷeṉak koḷḷumāṟṟāṉ mēlniṉṟu meykūṟum kēḷirākiya tāyarip pōlak kaḻaṟi avaṉ maṉakkavaliyi māṟṟip paṇṭu pōla maṉaṅkōṭal; āymaṉik kiḻattikkum uritteṉa moḻipa - ārāynta maṉiyaṟa nikaḻttuṅ kiḻattikkum uritteṉṟu kūṟupa; kavavoṭu mayaṅkiya kāliyāṉa - avaṉ muyakkattāl mayaṅkiya kālattu (e - ṟu.)
eṉṟatu, talivaṉ tavaṟṟiṟku uṭampaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉami kaṇṭa talivi ataṟku āṟṟātu taṉ maṉattup pulaviyellām māṟṟi itaṟkoṇṭum iṉiyi yākaleṉat 2 taḻīik koṇṭami kūṟiṟṟu. talivaṉ taṉ kuṇattiṉum ivaḷ kuṇam mikutikaṇṭu makiḻavē talivi taṉṉip pukaḻnta kuṟippu uṭiya ḷeṉpatūuṅ koḷka.	(32)
āyvuri :	itu talivikkuriyatōr iyalpuṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) īṉṟa tāyiṉip pōṉṟu talivaṉi iṭitturittu, avaṉatu maṉakkavaliyi māṟṟi aṉpiṉāl taḻuvik koḷḷutalum ārāytaliyuṭiya maṉiyaṟakkiḻami pūṇṭa talivikkum uriyatākum eṉpar; avaṉ taṉṉi muyaṅki makiḻuṅkālattu. e---ṟu.
tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīik kōṭal eṉṟatu, tāṉ peṟṟa makkaḷ aṟiyāmiyāṟ kuṟṟañceytavaḻi avarkaḷi veṟuttu vilakkātu iṭitturittu, mēlum ittakiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷic ceyyaṟka eṉṟu aṟivurikūṟi avartam maṉattuyarimāṟṟi muṉpōl aṉpiṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷutalākum.
taṉkaṇavaṉukku uṇṭākum naṉṟum tītum ārāyntu tītakaṟṟi naṉṟiṉpāl oḻukumpaṭi maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttavalla maṉivi eṉpār. āymaṉikkiḻatti, eṉṟār.
cuvavoṭumayaṅkiya kāli eṉṟatu, talivaṉ mayakkattāl taliviyuṭaṉ uḷaṅkalantu kūṭiyirukkum kālattiṉi. tāypōṟ kaḻaṟutal muṟkuṟitta vaṇṇam kāmakkiḻattiyark kēyaṉṟi maṉik kiḻattiyākiya talivikkum uriyatākum eṉpār maṉikkiḻattikkum eṉa ummi koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.
33.	avaṉcōrvu kāttal kaṭaṉeṉap paṭutaliṉ
makaṉtā yuyarpun taṉṉuyar pākuñ
celvaṉ paṇimoḻi iyalpāka lāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam :	ituvum talimakaṭkuriya kiḷaviyuṇart tiṟṟu. mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi :
(i - ḷ) talivaṉatu cōrvukāttal talimakaṭkuk kaṭaṉākalāl taṉmakaṉukkut tāyākiya kāmakkiḻatti yuyarpun taṉṉuyarpākum; iruvarun talimakaṉ paṇitta moḻikōṭal iyalpākalāṉ ; eṉṟavāṟu.1
cōrvāvatu oḻukkattiṟcōrvu. atu parattiyiṟpirivu. ataṉi maṟiyātu kāmakkiḻatti yāṟṟiṉ ṟalimakaṟkuk kuṟipāṭum varum eṉpataṉāl, avaḷi yuyartti yavvoḻukkam piṟarkkup pulaṉākāmi yēṟṟuk kōṭal vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟutalun taṉakku iḻipu ākātu uyarcciyām eṉṟavāṟu, itu mēlataṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟu.	(33)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	ituvun talivi kuṇac ciṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṉ cōrpu kāttal kaṭaṉ1 eṉappaṭutaliṉ-tāṉ nikaḻttukiṉṟa illaṟattāl talivaṟku iḻukkam piṟavāmaṟ pātukāttal talivikkuk kaṭappāṭeṉṟu kūṟappaṭutalāl, makaṉ tāy uyarpum taṉ uyarpu ākum - makaṉ tāyākiya māṟṟāḷit taṉṉiṉ iḻintāḷākak karutātu taṉṉōṭu oppa uyarntāḷākak koṇṭoḻukutal taṉatu uyarcciyām; celvaṉ paṇimoḻi iyalpu ākalāṉa - talivaṉ ivvā ṟoḻukukaveṉṟu tamakkup paṇitta moḻi nūlilakkaṇattāṉ āṉa moḻiyākalāṉ (e - ṟu).
īṇṭu makaṉṟā yeṉṟatu piṉmuṟiyākkiya vatuvi yāḷi. iṉṉum avaṉ cōrpu kāttal taṉakkuk kaṭaṉeṉṟu kūṟappaṭutalālē muṉmuṟiyākkiya vatuviyāḷit tammiṉ uyarntāḷeṉṟum vaḻipāṭāṟṟutalum piṉmuṟi vatuvi yāḷukku uyarpāñ celvaṉ paṇitta moḻiyāleṉṟavāṟu. īṇṭu makaṉṟā yeṉṟatu uyarntāḷi, uyttukkoṇṭuṇarta leṉṉu muttiyāl ivi yiraṇṭum poruḷ.2 celva ṉeṉṟār, paṉmakkaḷiyun taṉṉāṇi vaḻiyilē iruttun tiruvuṭimipaṟṟi. ivi vanta ceyyuṭkaḷ uyttuṇarka	(33)
āyvuri :	itu, mēṟkūṟiyataṉkaṇ eḻuvatōr aiyam akaṟṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i---ḷ) talivaṉatu vāḻkkiyiṟ cōrvu piṟavāmaṟ kāttal vāḻkkittuṇiviyākiya taliviyiṉ kaṭamiyeṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭutalāl tāy pōṉṟu talivaṉi iṭitturikkum uyarviṉi makaṉippeṟṟa talivi peṟṟiruttalum talivaṉatu uyarcciyākavē karutappeṟum : ellāc celvaṅkaḷukkum urimiyuṭiyaṉākiya talivaṉ, taṉpāl aṉpuṭiyārkaṇ paṇinta moḻiyiṉaṉāka oḻukutal avaṉukkuriya iyalpākalāṉ e---ṟu.
celvaṉ paṇimoḻi iyalpākalāṉ, makaṉtāy uyarpum taṉ uyarpākum eṉa iyiyum. celvaṉ paṇimoḻi iyal pākalāṉa eṉavarum ittoṭarpporuḷi viritturikkum muṟiyil amintatē,
		ellārkkum naṉṟām paṇital avaruḷḷuñ
celvarkakē celvan takittu	(125)
eṉavarum tirukkuṟaḷākum. kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭa nilimikkaṇ tāypōla muṉṉiṉṟu kaṇṭittaṟkuriya takuti maṉivikkum uṇṭu eṉpataṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvār, maṉikkiḻatti eṉṉātu makaṉtāy eṉat tāyān taṉmiyiṉik kuṟittār. taṉṉuyar pākum eṉpuḻit taṉ eṉṟatu talivaṉi.
34.	eṇṇarum pācaṟip peṇṇoṭum puṇarār.1
iḷampūraṇam :	itu talimakkaṭ kuriyatoru marapu uṇarttiṟṟu.
(i---ḷ.) niṉittaṟkariya pācaṟikkaṇ talimakaḷirōṭum puṇarār talimakkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
niṉittaṟkarumi - māṟṟāri velluṅ karuttu mēṟ kōṭaliṟ ṟalimakaḷiri niṉikkalākātāyiṟṟu. pācaṟi eṉa vicēṭittavataṉāl ēṉip pirivukkumāmeṉṟu koḷka.	(34)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; munnīr vaḻakkam (tol. akat. 34) eṉpataṉāṟ pakitaṇi viṉikkuṅ kāvaṟkuṅ kaṭumpoṭu1 cēṟalāmeṉṟu eytiyataṉi vilakkaliṉ.
(i - ḷ) eṇ arum pācaṟi - pōr ceytu vellumāṟṟi eṇṇum ariya pācaṟiyiṭattu; peṇṇoṭu puṇarār-taliviyaroṭu talivaṉikkūṭṭip pulaneṟivaḻakkañ ceyyār (e - ṟu) 2.
iravum pakalum pōrttoḻiṉ māṟāmi tōṉṟa arum pācaṟi yeṉṟār.
		naḷḷeṉ yāmattum paḷḷi koḷḷāṉ
cilaroṭu tiritarum vēntaṉ
palaroṭu muraṇiya pācaṟit toḻilē
	(pattu. neṭunal. 186 - 188)
eṉavum,
		oruki paḷḷi yoṟṟi yoruki
muṭiyoṭu kaṭakañ cērtti neṭitu niṉintu
	(pattu-mulli. 75, 76)
eṉavum varuvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aritāka uñaṟṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉik kāvaṟpirivukku muṟiceytu kāppāṟṟutali eṇṇu meṉap poruḷurikka. 3	(34)
āyvuri :	itu pōrttoḻil mēṟkoṇṭa tali makkaṭku uriyatōr iyalpuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) pakivariveṉṟaṭakkutaṟkuriya neṟimuṟikaḷi ārāytaṟkuriya ariya iṭamākiya pācaṟiyiṉkaṇ makaḷiroṭu uḷḷam poruntār (talimakkaḷ) e---ṟu.
pōrkkaḷattil tam paṭimaṟavarkaḷum, yāṉi kutiriyākiya cēṉikaḷum āṟṟiya pōrc ceyalkaḷiyum avaṟṟāl irutiṟappaṭikaḷukkum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻivukaḷiyum eṇṇip pakivari veṉṟaṭakkutaṟku iṉi nām mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya naṭi muṟikaḷ yāviyeṉa maṉam oṉṟi ārāyntu tuṇitaṟkuriya iṭamāy nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻkkiyiṉi uruvākkum arumiyuṭiyatu pācaṟiyātalāl, attakiya cūḻccittiṟattiṟku iṭaṉāyaminta pācaṟikkaṇṇē uḷḷattiyīrkkum kātal makaḷiroṭum maṉattāl poruntutalum uṭaṉuṟitalum kūṭār eṉa aṟivuṟuttum niliyil amintatu iccūttiramākum.
pōriṟ pakivari vellutaṟkuriya neṟimuṟikaḷi eṇṇi ārāytaṟkuriya arumiyuṭiyatu pācaṟi eṉpataṉi,

eṉa nakkīraṉārum kūṟiya pācaṟi nikaḻaccikaḷ, eṇṇarum pācaṟi eṉavarum ittolkāppiyat toṭarkku uriya viḷakkamāka amintuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkkiyuṇarattakuvatākum.
maṉivāḻkkikkuriya peṇmit taṉmiyiṉarāya makaḷir eṉpatupaṭap peṇ eṉṟār.puṇarār eṉpuḻip puṇartal eṉṉuñcol iṇiviḻicciṉic cuṭṭātu maṉattāṟporuntu talic cuṭṭiniṉṟatu.
35.	puṟantō rāṅkaṭ puṇarva tākum.
iḷampūraṇam :	itu mēlataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi.
(i---ḷ)  mēṟ collappaṭṭa pācaṟikkaṭ puṟappeṇṭir puṇarcci poruntuvatu eṉṟavāṟu.
poruntuvatu eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṭṭameṉṟu koḷka. avarāvār tātiyaruṅ kaṇikiyarum.	
35.	puṟattōr āṅkaṇ puriva teṉpa
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	iḵtu eytiyatu ikantu paṭāmaṟ kāttatu.
(i-ḷ) puṟattōr āṅkaṇ- aṭiyōrum viṉivalayāṅki ṉōrumākiya akappuṟat talivaruṭiya  1 pācaṟiyiṭattāyil; purivatu eṉpa-avarip peṇṇōṭu puṇarttup pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañ ceytal poruntuvatu eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar (e-ṟu).
ip pācaṟippirivi variyaṟuppavē ēṉip pirivukaḷukkup puṇarttalum puṇarāmiyum puṟattōrkku variviṉṟāyiṟṟu.
āyvuri:	itu, mēṟkūṟiyataṟtōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) puṟattiṇip paṇikaḷukkuriya peṇṭirāyiṉ pācaṟiyil taṅkiyiruttal poruntuvatākum eṉpar  āciriyar e-ṟu.
puṟattōr eṉṟatu. maṉivāḻkkiyākiya akattiṇit toṭarpiṉṟi pōrttoḻilutaviyākiyapuṟattiṇippaṇiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa puṟappeṇṭiri. pōriṟ puṇpaṭṭa vīrārkku maruntiṭṭup pēṇutalum pātukāttalum, eṇṇeyppīli ēntik kiyiṟ pantaṅ koṇṭu pācaṟiyil viḷakkēṟṟi avi iravu muḻuvatum aviyāmaṟ pārttuk koḷḷutalum mutalākavuḷḷa pōrttoḻilutavip paṇikaḷ pācaṟiyil uṟiyum puṟap peṇṭirkkuriyaṉavākum. pācaṟiyiṟ puṟappeṇṭirkkuriya paṇikaḷ cilavaṟṟi,
		kuṟuntoṭi muṉkik kūntalañ ciṟupuṟat
tiravupakaṟ ceyyun tiṇpiṭiyoḷvāḷ
viravuvarik kacciṟ pūṇṭamaṅkiyar
neyyumiḻ curiyar neṭuntirik koḷīi
kiyami viḷakkam nantutoṟu māṭṭa
	(mullippāṭṭu---45-49)
eṉa nappūtaṉār virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum.
purivateṉpa eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar koṇṭa pāṭam. purivatu---poruntuvatu. puṇarvateṉpa eṉappāṭaṅ koṇṭu, pācaṟikkaṭ puṟappeṇṭir puṇarcci poruntuvatu eṉap poruḷ varintu avarāvār tātiyaruṅkaṇikiyarum eṉa viḷakkam taruvar iḷampūraṇar. māṟṟār cēṉikaḷi veṉṟaṭakkum pōrttoḻiṟ ṟiṟaṅkaḷik kūrntu cintittu ārāytaṟku nilikkaḷimākiya ariya pācaṟiyilē avvārāyccikku ūṟuviḷikkum niliyil kāmamayakkatti viḷikkum kaṇikiyaroṭum tātiyaroṭum kūṭik kāmanukartal eṉpatu evvāṟṟāṉum poruntātu. eṉavē iṅkup puṟattōr eṉaccuṭṭappaṭṭōr pōrttoḻilutavippaṇi makaḷirākiya vīrappeṇṭir eṉak koḷḷutalē ēṟpuṭiyatākum. ākavē, puṇarvatu eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkum poruntuvatu eṉapporuḷ koḷḷutalē āciriyar karuttukku ēṟpuṭiyatākum.
iṉi, puṟattōr. eṉpataṟku aṭiyōrum viṉivala pāṅkiṉōrum ākiya akappuṟattalivaruṭiya pācaṟiyiṭattāyiṉ avarip peṇṇoṭu puṇarttup pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañ ceytal poruntuvatu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟum poruḷum, poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟavilli; ulakiyaliṟpuṟat tiṇi yoḻukalāṟākiya pōrttoḻil nikaḻttum talivarkaḷi aṉpiṉintiṇikkuriya talivarkaḷ eṉṟum kikkiḷi peruntiṇikkuriya akappuṟattalivar eṉṟum pirittukkūṟal marapiṉmiyāṉum, pācaṟiyil uḷḷōr yāvarāyiṉum tām pakivari veṉṟaṭakkutaṟkuriya pōrttiṟaṅkaḷi ārāytaṟkuriya pācaṟiyil akavāḻkkikkuriya makaḷiroṭu taṅkiyiruttalō avarkaḷi maṉattāl eṇṇutalō kūṭāteṉa aṟivuṟuttalē mēlic cūttirattiṉ nōkkamātalāṉum īṇṭup puṟattōr eṉṟatu akavāḻkki yuṇarviṉṟip pōrt toḻilutaviyākiya puṟattiṇik kaṭamiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa vīra makaḷirik kuṟittatu eṉak koḷḷutalē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukkum caṅka vilakkiyaṅ kūṟum pōr neṟi marapiṟkum ēṟpuṭiyatākum.
36.	kāmanili yurittalum tērnili yurittaluṅ
kiḻavōṉ kuṟippiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟalum*
āvoṭu paṭṭa nimittaṅ kūṟalum
celavuṟu kiḻaviyum celavaḻuṅku kiḷaviyum
aṉṉavi piṟavum pārppārk kuriya.
iḷam pūraṇam :	itu, pārppārkkuriya kiḷavi yuṇarttiṟṟu.
(i---ḷ.) kāmanili yurittalāvatu---nī piriyiṉ ivaḷ kāmamikum eṉṟu kūṟutal.
aṟaṉiṉṟi ayaltūṟṟum ampali eṉpataṉuḷ.
		uṭiyiva ḷuyirvāḻāḷ nīnīppiṉ eṉappala
iṭikoṇṭiyām irappavum emakoḷḷāy āyiṉi
kaṭiiya vāṟṟiṭi nīrnītta vaṟuñcuṉi
aṭiyoṭu vāṭiya aṇimalar takippaṉa	(kalit. 3)
eṉavarum.
tērnili yurittalāvatu---ārāycci niliyāṟ kūṟutal.1 atu varumāṟu:-
	vēṉiluḻantavaṟituyaṅkōykaḷiṟu......
muyaṉṟu ceyporuḷē	(kali--7)
kiḻavōṉ kuṟippiṉi yeṭuttuk kūṟalum eṉpatu--talivaṉ kuṟippiṉit talivikku viḷaṅkak kūṟalum eṉṟavāṟu.
āvoṭu paṭṭa nimittaṅ kūṟutalum eṉpatu---āvoṭu paṭṭanimittaṅ kūṟalum eṉṟavāṟu. paṭṭa nimittam eṉṟataṉāl ellā nimittamuṅ koḷka.
celavuṟu kiḷavi eṉpatu---celavuṟṟa collum eṉṟavāṟu. aḵtāvatu talimakaṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal ivaṟṟiṟku ilakkiyam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
celavaḻuṅku kiḷavi eṉpatu---celavaḻuṅkal vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟutal.
naṭuvikantorīi......mJkD« poruḷē	(kalit.8)
eṉavarum.
ivi pārppārk kuriya veṉṟavāṟu. ōrupāṟ kiḷaviyēṉip pāṟkaṇṇum (poruḷiyal - 27) varumeṉpataṉāl tōḻi māṭṭum pāṅkaṉ māṭṭum koḷka.1	(36)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam :	itu, pārppārkkuriya kiḷavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ.) kāmanili urittalum---talivaṉatu kāmamikuti kaṇṭu itaṉ nili iṟṟeṉṟu iḻittuk kūṟuvaṉavum; tērnili urittalum --- aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟi avaṉ tērumāṟu ētuvum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṅ kūṟalum; kiḻavōṉ kuṟippiṉi eṭuttaṉar moḻitalum - talivaṉ tāḻntoḻukiyavaṟṟi avaṉ kuṟippāṉ aṟintu veḷippaṭutti avaṟkē kūṟutalum; āvoṭupaṭṭa nimittam kūṟalum---vēḷvikkapili pāṟpayaṅkuṉṟutalāṉuṅ, kuṉṟātu kalaniṟiyap poḻitalāṉum; uḷatāya nimittam paṟṟit talivaṟku varum naṉmi tīmi kūṟutalum; celavuṟu kiḷaviyum---avaṉ piriyuṅkāl naṉṉimittam paṟṟic celavu naṉṟeṉṟu kūṟutalum; celavu aḻuṅku kiḷaviyum--tīyanimittam paṟṟic celavit tavirttuk kūṟutalum; aṉṉa piṟavum avi pōlvaṉa piṟavum; pārppārkku uriya--antaṇarkku1 uriya (e---ṟu.)
tērnili yeṉṟataṉāl tērntu piṉṉum kalaṅkiṉuṅ kalaṅkāmal teḷivunili kāṭṭaluṅ koḷka. aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ. aṟivar iṭittuk kūṟiyāṅkut tāmum iṭittuk kūṟuvaṉavum, koḷka. moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟātaṉavum īṇṭē kūṟiṉār. aḵtu ippēraṟivu uṭiyiyāyiṉ iṉiyiyākaṟpāli yalliyeṉakkāmanili yurittaluṅ kaṟpiṉuḷ illiruntu makiḻvōṟ killiyāṟ pukaḻē eṉat talivaṉ niṉiyumāṟṟāṟ kāmanili yurittalum aṭaṅkiṟṟu. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum iruvakik kikōḷiṟkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka pārppāṉ pāṅkaṉ eṉa uṭaṉ kūṟiṉamiyiṟ pāṅkaṟkum ēṟpaṉavum koḷka. iviyellān taliccaṅkattārum iṭic caṅkattāruñ ceyta pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa pōlum. ikkālattil ilakkiyamiṉṟu pāṅkaṉ2 kūṟuvaṉa nōymaruṅ kaṟinaru ḷaṭakkik koṇṭu eṭuttu moḻiyappaṭutalaṉṟik kūṟṟu avaṇ iṉmi uṇarka. atu,
		vēṭṭōr tiṟattu virumpiyaniṉ pākaṉum
nīṭṭittā yeṉṟu kaṭāṅkaṭun tiṇṭēr
pūṭṭu viṭāa niṟuttu	(kali. 66)
eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷil itu pōla varuvaṉa piṟa aṉittum uyttuṇarntu koḷka.	(36)
āyvuri :	itu, pārppārkkuriya kiḷavikaḷit tokuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) kātalariṭiyē vēṭki niliyiṉi eṭut turittalum talivaṉ ūrntu varum tēriṉ nilimiyik kūṟutalum, talivaṉatu uḷḷakkuṟippiṉit talivikku viḷaṅkak kūṟutalum, pacu mutalākap  poruntiya naṉṉimittaṅkaḷi uṇarntu kūṟutalum, talimakaṉ pirintu ceṉṟa ceytiyik kūṟutalum, piriyakkarutiya talivaṉi nōkki, nī cellātu cila nāṭkaḷ tāmatittal vēṇṭum eṉakkūṟutalum avipōlvaṉapiṟavum pārppārkkuriya kūṟṟukkaḷākum. e-ṟu.
celavu aḻuṅkutal- pirintu cellakkarutiya talivaṉ uṭaṉē puṟappaṭātu cilanāṭkaḷ tāmatittal. tērniliyurittal eṉpataṟku ārāycci niliyāṟ kūṟutal eṉa  iḷampūraṇarum, talivaṉ tērumāṟu ētuvum eṭuttukkāṭṭuṅkūṟal eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum poruḷ koḷvar. nallatu tērntu koḷka.
37.	ellā vāyiṉum iruvar tēettum
pulliya makiḻccip poruḷa eṉpa.
iḷampūraṇam:	itu vāyilkaṭ kuḷḷatoru marapu uṇarttiṟṟu. 
(i---ḷ) vāyilkaḷ ellām iruvar māṭṭum poruntiya makiḻccip poruṇmiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu. 1
iruvarāvār talivaṉun taliviyum. eṉavē vekuṭcip poruṇmi kūṟappeṟār eṉṟavāṟu.	(37)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	itu vāyilkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellāvāyilum---pārppāṉ mutaliya vāyil kaḷellām; iruvar tēettum pulliya--talivaṉ kaṇṇun talivikaṇṇum poruntiya; makiḻccip poruḷ eṉpa- maṉamakiḻccip poruḷiṉi nikaḻttutalit tamakkup poruḷākavuṭiyar (e---ṟu).1
eṉavē, makiḻcci kūṟappeṟārāyiṟṟu, 2 pulliya, eṉṟataṉāṉē viruntum putalvarum āṟṟāmiyum vāyilākupa eṉṟu koḷka. vāyilkaḷ tōḻi tāyē (tol,poruḷ, 193) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟupa. cjhuz« tªJÊ¡ fh©f.(37)

38.	aṉputalip pirinta 1 kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉa
ciṟippuṟaṅ kuṟittaṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḷampūraṇam:	itu mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi.
(i---ḷ) vāyilkaṇ makiḻccip poruṇmi kūṟutalaṉṟiyaṉpu nīṅkiya kiḷavi kūṟiṉārāyiṟ ṟalivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākap peṟuvar eṉac colluvar eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum ōrilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	itu, makiḻccip poruḷaṉṟi vāyilkaḷ ivvāṟu kūṟappeṟuvar eṉṟaliṉ eytiya tikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttatu.
(i---ḷ) aṉpu talippirinta kiḷavi 2 tōṉṟiṉ- aṉpu iruvariṭattum nīṅkiya kaṭuñcol av vāyilkaḷiṭattut tōṉṟumākil; ciṟippuṟaṅ kuṟittaṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulavar--- oruvarkkoruvar ciṟippuṟat tārākak 3 kūṟalvēṇṭu meṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar (e---ṟu).
tōṉṟi ṉeṉpatu paṭittuk koṇṭu kūṟuvareṉ patāmākaliṉ, kuṟittaṉṟu eṉpatu pōyiṉṟu eṉpatupōla ṟakaram ūrnta kuṟṟiyalukaram aṟiyāmiyiṉ, eṉṉum (50) naṟṟiṇippāṭṭum utāraṇamām; atu ciṟip puṟamākavuṅ koḷḷak kiṭantamiyiṉ.	(38)
āyvuri:	itu, mēlataṟkōr. puṟaṉaṭi.
(i---ḷ) talivaṉ taliviyākiya avviruvariṭattum aṉpu nīṅkiya kaṭuñcoṟkaḷik kūṟavēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi nērntāl, akkūṟṟu nērniṉṟu kūṟappaṭātu, ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟum kuṟippiṉatāka amital vēṇṭum e---ṟu.
tōṉṟiṉ eṉṉum ceyiṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi yeccam aṅṅaṉantōṉṟutal aritu eṉpataṉippulappaṭuttiyatu, kuṟittaṉṟu- kuṟittatu.
vaṉpuṟi kuṟitta kiḷaviyellām
aṉputalippirinta kiḷavitōṉṟiṉ
ciṟippuṟaṅkuṟitataṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulavar.
	(iṟiyaṉār -53)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaṟ cūttiram ittolkāppiya nūṟpāvi avvāṟē taṉṉakattu eṭuttāṇṭuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkkiyuṇartaṟkuriyatākum.
39.	taṟpukaḻ kiḷavi kiḻavaṉmuṟ kiḷattal
ettiṟat tāṉum kiḻattik killi
muṟpaṭa vakutta iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē.
iḷampūraṇam:	itu talivik kuriyatōr marapuṇarttiṟṟu.
(i---ḷ) talivaṉ muṉṉart taṉṉip pukaḻuṅ kūṟṟu evvaḻiyāṉuṅ kiḻattikku illi; muṟpaṭak kūṟiya iraṇṭiṭamum 1 allāta vaḻi yeṉṟavāṟu.
aviyāvaṉa talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantavaḻi irattalun teḷittalum eṉa akattiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟiya iraṇṭum. avvaḻip pukaḻtalāvatu:
orūunī emkūntal koḷḷal yām niṉṉi
verūutuṅ kāṇuṅ kaṭi	(kalit.78)
eṉṟavaḻit taṉṉi uyarttuk kūṟutalāṟ pukaḻntāḷām; niṉṉi veruvātār piṟar eṉṉum uḷḷakkaruttiṉāl.
		nī kūṟum poyccūḷ aṇaṅkākiṉ maṟṟiṉi
yārmēl viḷiyumō kūṟu	(kalit. 88)
eṉṟavaḻiyum peṇṭir palar uḷarāyiṉum avar ellār māṭṭuñ cellātu taṉmēl varumeṉak kuṟittāḷātaliṉ, taṉṉip pukaḻntāḷām. piṟavumaṉṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam:	itu, taliviyilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) taṟpukaḻ kiḷavi kiḻavaṉmuṉ kiḷattal---taṉṉip pukaḻnturittalik kiḻavaṉ muṉṉarc collutal; ettiṟattāṉuṅ kiḻattikku illi-naṉimiku cīṟṟattuṉiyiṉum talivikku illi; muṟpaṭa vakutta iraṇṭalaṅkaṭiyē-muṉpu kūṟupaṭuttōtiya tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīik kōṭalum avaṉ cōrpu kāttaṟku makaṉṟā yuyarpu taṉṉuyar pātalum  allāviṭattu (e-ṟu).
kiḻattikkilliyeṉa muṭikka, avviraṇṭiṭattun taṉatu kuṇacciṟappik kuṟippāl talivaṉmuṉṉē pukaḻvāḷpōla oḻukiṉāḷeṉ ṟuṇarka.
iṉi muṟpaṭavakutta iraṇṭu eṉpataṟku, irattalun teḷittalum (tol.akat 41) eṉa akattiṇiyiyaluṟā kūṟiyaṉaveṉṟumām. 1 talivaṉ muṉṉar illiyeṉavē avaṉ muṉṉar allātaviṭattup pukaḻtal peṟṟām. avi kāmak kiḻattiyarum avarkkup pāṅkāyiṉāruṅ kēṭpap pukaḻtalām. 
		pēṇutaku ciṟappiṟ peṇṇiyal pāyiṉum
eṉṉōṭu puriyuna vallaḷ
taṉṉōṭu puriyunart tāṉaṟi kunaḷē.
eṉap patiṟṟuppattil vantatu.
āyvuri:	itu, maṉiyaṟattiṉi nikaḻttum talivikku iṉṟiyamiyātatōr tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i---ḷ) talivaṉ muṉṉart taṉṉip pukaḻnturikkuṅ kūṟṟu evvakiyālum talivikku illi, muṉṉar vakutturitta iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷum allātaviṭattu e---ṟu.
muṉṉar vakutturitta iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷāvaṉa: talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantavaḻi talimakaḷi aṇuki irattalum teḷittalum eṉa muṉṉar akattiṇi 44 ām cūttirattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, tāypōṟ cuḻaṟittaḻīkkōṭalum  (34) eṉa ivviyalil muṟkūṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kuṟippiṭuvar. muṟpaṭa vakutta iraṇṭu eṉpataṟku irattalum teḷittalum eṉa akat tiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟiyaṉaveṉṟumām eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iḷampūraṇar karuttiṉiyum ēṟṟukkoḷḷutalāl, parattiyiṟ pirintu vantuḻit talivaṉ taliviyi irattalum teḷittalum ākiya iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉak koḷḷutalē poruttamuṭiyatākum. ivviraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷilum talivi taṉṉippukaḻtalum takum eṉpatu karuttu.
ittolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷi uḷaṅkoṇṭu kiḻatti kiḻavōṉ muṉṉart taṉṉip pukaḻtal pulavikkālattilum takkataṉṟu eṉa vaṟpuṟuttum muṟiyil amintatu,
		kiḻavōṉ muṉṉark kiḻatti taṟpukaḻtal
pulavik kālattum purivataṉṟē.	(37)
eṉavarum iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyaṟ cūttiramākum.
40.	kiḻavi muṉṉart taṟpukaḻ kiḷavi
kiḻavōṉ viṉivayiṉ uriya eṉpa.
iḷampūraṇam:	itu, talivaṟ kuriyatoru marapuṇarttiṟṟu.
(i---ḷ) talivi muṉṉart talivaṉ ṟaṉṉip pukaḻuṅ kūṟṟu viṉivayiṟ piriyum vaḻi yuriya eṉṟavāṟu. 1 
illeṉa irantōrkkoṉ ṟīyāmi iḻivu	(kalit. 3)
eṉṟum,

(i---ḷ) kiḻavi muṉṉark kiḻavōṉ taṟpukaḻ kiḷavi- talivimuṉṉart talivaṉ taṉṉip pukaḻntu kūṟuṅ kūṟṟu; viṉivayiṉ uriya eṉpa- kāriyaṅkaḷi nikaḻttuṅ kāraṇattiṭattu 2 uriyaveṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar 	(e---ṟu.)
ak kāraṇamāvaṉa, kalviyuṅ, koṭiyum, poruḷceyalum, muṟṟakappaṭṭōṉi3 muṟṟuviṭuttalumākiya4 kāriyaṅkaḷi nikaḻntuvaleṉak kūṟuvaṉa. iv vāḷviṉic ciṟappi yāṉ eytuvaleṉat taṉṉip pukaḻavē atupaṟṟit talivi pirivāṟṟutal payaṉāyiṟṟu.
illeṉa irantōrkkoṉ ṟīyāmi yiḷiveṉa 			(kali.2)
